📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期呼吸道合胞病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 在 尼塞韦单抗 抗原位点内检测到的大多数替换反映了持续的天然 RSV 进化,并且不会显着影响 尼塞韦单抗 的敏感性。然而,涉及残基 64-68 和 204-208 的多个 RSV-B 取代和组合显示出敏感性降低或高水平耐药性。
  • 该审查确定了美国 RSV、流感和 SARS-CoV-2 的 13 个 监测 数据源,其中除一个外均来自政府。它强调了高质量的 监测 数据对于公共卫生干预措施的重要性,并概述了这些系统收集的数据。
  • 92例RSV抗原阳性率为45.7%,平均年龄4.18±3.08个月。抗原阳性病例中,重症毛细支气管炎病例更为常见(22.8%),且住院时间最长。11 月(42.4%)和 12 月(29.3%)期间,RSV 感染水平达到峰值。这项研究描述,在季节性流行期间,RSV 是主要问题,因为在接下来的几个月里,阳性病例数量有所减少。
  • 队列研究共纳入4,416名患者,其中1,366例为RSV感染,3,050例为甲型流感。在所有随访期内,RSV患者的死亡率均高于甲型流感患者。调整后的风险比(aHR)在所有随访期内,RSV患者的死亡率也均高于甲型流感患者。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Most substitutions detected within the nirsevimab antigenic site reflect ongoing natural RSV evolution and do not significantly affect nirsevimab susceptibility.
  • Meta-analyses of RCTs showed that maternal RSVpreF vaccines lowered medically attended RSV-related lower respiratory tract infections with vaccine efficacy of 68% (relative risk (RR) 0.32; 95% confidence interval (CI) 0.13 to 0.77, 2 trials; 7,656 participants; high certainty of evidence (CoE)), severe RSV-related lower respiratory tract infections with a vaccine efficacy of 84% (RR 0.16; 95% CI 0.07 to 0.36, 2 trials; 7,656 participants, high CoE) and RSV-related hospitalisations with a vaccine efficacy of 70% (RR 0.30; 95% CI 0.15 to 0.61, 1 trial; 7,148 participants, high CoE) in infants.
  • The review identifies thirteen surveillance data sources for RSV, influenza, and SARS-CoV-2 in the U.S., with all but one being governmental.
  • RSV antigen positivity was detected in 45.7% of the 92 cases, with a mean age of 4.18±3.08 months. Severe bronchiolitis cases were more common (22.8%) among antigen positive cases and had the longest hospital stay.

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期呼吸道合胞病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • CDC 发布了一份关于呼吸道病毒活动水平的报告,其中包括 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和其他相关病原体。 [news-e061dda159c8e5a3]。
  • 《药物话题》报道称,尼塞韦单抗对呼吸道合胞病毒的保护作用在第二年减弱。文章讨论了该药物效果随时间推移而减弱的问题。 [news-c5a66d62a9e6334c]。
  • 发表于《美国医学会杂志·儿科学》的一项全国性尼塞韦单抗研究发现,尼塞韦单抗对RSV下呼吸道感染相关住院的保护作用在第二年随访中减弱,但在两次免疫活动的首年显示出显著效果。 [news-7c3f5c94354cbf9d]。
  • 石药集团重组RSV 疫苗(SYS6057)获得FDA批准在美国开始临床试验。这是该公司报告的已确认事件。 [news-9f56b4a2fd9ee8a3]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Respiratory syncytial virus news developments.
  • The CDC published a report on respiratory virus activity levels, including Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) and other related pathogens. [news-e061dda159c8e5a3].
  • Nirsevimab's RSV protection fades by the second year, as reported by Drug Topics. The article discusses the diminishing effectiveness of the drug over time. [news-c5a66d62a9e6334c].
  • A nationwide cohort study published in JAMA Pediatrics found that nirsevimab's protective effect against RSV-LRTI-related hospitalization faded in the second year of follow-up, but it showed strong effectiveness during the first year across two immunization campaigns.
  • CSPC Pharmaceutical Group's recombinant RSV vaccine, SYS6057, received FDA approval to begin clinical trials in the United States. This is a confirmed event reported by the company. [news-9f56b4a2fd9ee8a3].

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: BMJ Open Respiratory Research  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-20  |  DOI: 10.1136/bmjresp-2025-003996  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 13, 1, e003996
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

3岁以下儿童在英格兰各级医疗保健机构中呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)负担较高。

High respiratory syncytial virus burden in children under 3 years of age across all care levels in England.
作者： Emma C Carter, Helen Hill, Carla Solórzano, Lauren Kerruish, Lauren McLellan, James Dodd, Adam B. Smith, Andria Joseph, Damian Lewis, Fred Fyles
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项前瞻性研究旨在估计英国3岁以下儿童实验室确诊的呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)负担。
设计与对象
该研究在默西赛德和布里斯托尔实施,涵盖11个初级保健机构、5个步行诊所、2所二级保健医院和2所三级保健医院。纳入了出现下呼吸道感染(LRTI)症状的3岁以下儿童,其中初级保健机构的子研究纳入了患有上呼吸道感染(URTI)的儿童。
核心方法
主要和次要结果指标:本研究的主要结果是RSV感染在初级、二级和三级医疗保健机构中的发生率。次要结果包括严重程度指标(住院率和高依赖护理入院率)、严重疾病的风险因素、疾病经济负担以及合并感染情况。
主要结果
2000 children were included in the analysis (410 primary care and 1590 secondary/tertiary care). RSV prevalence was 50.0% (95% CI 46.7% to 53.3%) in children admitted to hospital via emergency departments (ED), 36.3% (95% CI 31.7% to 41.0%) in ED discharges, 36.5% (95% CI 24.7% to 49.6%) in primary care (LRTI) and 12.7% (95% CI 8.6% to 17.9%) in primary care URTI. Healthy term-born children accounted for 70.1% of RSV hospitalisations. RSV-positive cases incurred a higher mean cost per participant (£1811, 95% CI £1720 to £1903) than RSV-negative cases (£1295, 95% CI £1220 to £1370).
解释与创新
研究结果揭示了RSV带来的显著负担。即使是中度早产也是严重疾病的风险因素,这些儿童可能由于晚期妊娠抗体转移不足而无法从母体RSV抗体中获益。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果突显了RSV对婴幼儿的重大影响,并建议应考虑将中度早产儿童纳入母体RSV抗体的更广泛资格范围内。
局限与证据强度
该研究在英格兰特定地区进行,可能限制其普遍适用性。由于基于大样本量和实验室确诊病例,证据强度较强。

High respiratory syncytial virus burden in children under 3 years of age across all care levels in England.

Authors: Emma C Carter, Helen Hill, Carla Solórzano, Lauren Kerruish, Lauren McLellan, James Dodd, Adam B. Smith, Andria Joseph, Damian Lewis, Fred Fyles
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: This prospective surveillance study aimed to estimate the burden of laboratory-confirmed respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in under 3-year-olds in the United Kingdom. SETTING: The study was implemented in Merseyside and Bristol, encompassing 11 primary care sites, 5 walk-in centres, 2 secondary care hospitals and 2 tertiary care hospitals. PARTICIPANTS: Children aged under 3 years presenting with lower respiratory tract infection (LRTI) symptoms were included, with a substudy in primary care recruiting children with upper respiratory tract infection (URTI). PRIMARY AND SECONDARY OUTCOME MEASURES: The primary outcome of the study was the prevalence of RSV infection in primary, secondary and tertiary healthcare settings. Secondary outcomes included severity outcomes (rates of hospitalisation and high dependency care admissions), risk factors for severe disease, economic burden of disease and coinfection prevalence. RESULTS: 2000 children were included in the analysis (410 primary care and 1590 secondary/tertiary care). 318 were included in the URTI substudy. RSV prevalence was 50.0% (95% CI 46.7% to 53.3%) in children admitted to hospital via emergency departments (ED), 36.3% (95% CI 31.7% to 41.0%) in ED discharges, 36.5% (95% CI 24.7% to 49.6%) in primary care (LRTI) and 12.7% (95% CI 8.6% to 17.9%) in primary care URTI. Healthy term-born children accounted for 70.1% of RSV hospitalisations. Risk factors for severe disease included any level of prematurity, age <3 months and congenital cardiac disease. RSV-positive cases incurred a higher mean cost per participant (£1811, 95% CI £1720 to £1903) than RSV-negative cases (£1295, 95% CI £1220 to £1370). CONCLUSIONS: The findings revealed a substantial burden associated with RSV. Even moderate prematurity was a risk factor for severe disease, and these children may not benefit from maternal RSV vaccination due to missed late gestation antibody transfer. Furthermore, they would not be eligible for Nirsevimab under UK guidance, which supports consideration of broadening eligibility to include these children.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This prospective surveillance study aimed to estimate the burden of laboratory-confirmed respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in under 3-year-olds in the United Kingdom.
Design and population
The study was implemented in Merseyside and Bristol, encompassing 11 primary care sites, 5 walk-in centres, 2 secondary care hospitals and 2 tertiary care hospitals. Children aged under 3 years presenting with lower respiratory tract infection (LRTI) symptoms were included, with a substudy in primary care recruiting children with upper respiratory tract infection (URTI).
Core methods
PRIMARY AND SECONDARY OUTCOME MEASURES: The primary outcome of the study was the prevalence of RSV infection in primary, secondary and tertiary healthcare settings. Secondary outcomes included severity outcomes (rates of hospitalisation and high dependency care admissions), risk factors for severe disease, economic burden of disease and coinfection prevalence.
Main results
2000 children were included in the analysis (410 primary care and 1590 secondary/tertiary care). RSV prevalence was 50.0% (95% CI 46.7% to 53.3%) in children admitted to hospital via emergency departments (ED), 36.3% (95% CI 31.7% to 41.0%) in ED discharges, 36.5% (95% CI 24.7% to 49.6%) in primary care (LRTI) and 12.7% (95% CI 8.6% to 17.9%) in primary care URTI. Healthy term-born children accounted for 70.1% of RSV hospitalisations. RSV-positive cases incurred a higher mean cost per participant (£1811, 95% CI £1720 to £1903) than RSV-negative cases (£1295, 95% CI £1220 to £1370).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings revealed a substantial burden associated with RSV. Even moderate prematurity was a risk factor for severe disease, and these children may not benefit from maternal RSV vaccination due to missed late gestation antibody transfer.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight the significant impact of RSV on young children and suggest the need for broader eligibility criteria for maternal RSV vaccination to include those with moderate prematurity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in specific regions of England, which may limit generalisability. The evidence is strong as it is based on a large sample size and laboratory-confirmed cases.

老年人呼吸道合胞病毒感染

Respiratory Syncytial Virus in Older Adults
作者： Joan Somes
摘要中文翻译教育目标 教育活动结束后,参与者将能够描述 呼吸道合胞病毒 感染的 流行病学;讨论 65 岁及以上成年人以及患有高危疾病的年轻人的临床表现和结果;并讨论诊断挑战、当前的 治疗 选项以及即将推出的新 治疗 和预防选项。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在填补对呼吸道合胞病毒感染在65岁及以上成人以及高风险条件的年轻成人中的流行病学、临床表现及结局的认知缺口。
设计与对象
该研究为一项教育活动,旨在向参与者提供关于65岁及以上成人以及高风险条件的年轻成人中呼吸道合胞病毒感染的知识。
核心方法
这些方法涉及一项教育活动,向参与者提供有关 流行病学、临床表现、结果、诊断挑战、当前 治疗 选项以及新兴 治疗 和特定成人人群中 呼吸道合胞病毒 感染的预防策略的信息。
主要结果
结果显示,参与者能够描述呼吸道合胞病毒感染的流行病学,讨论其在65岁及以上成人及高风险条件的年轻成人中的临床表现和结局,并应对诊断挑战、当前抗病毒药物治疗选项以及新型治疗和预防选项。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为证明该教育活动在提升参与者对脆弱成人群体中呼吸道合胞病毒感染认知方面的有效性,并强调该方法在填补该领域知识缺口方面的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为制定针对医护专业人员的目标教育计划提供依据,以改善对老年人及高风险条件的年轻成人中呼吸道合胞病毒感染的识别与管理。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到其教育设计的限制,未提供临床结果或 治疗 疗效的经验数据。该证据基于教育活动,可能无法完全捕捉现实世界临床场景的复杂性。

Respiratory Syncytial Virus in Older Adults

Authors: Joan Somes
Original abstract (English)
EDUCATIONAL OBJECTIVES After the educational activity, participants will be able to describe the epidemiology of respiratory syncytial virus infection; discuss the clinical presentation and outcomes in adults aged 65 years and older as well as younger adults with high-risk conditions; and discuss diagnostic challenges, current treatment options, and new treatment and prevention options on the horizon.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the lack of understanding regarding the epidemiology, clinical presentation, and outcomes of respiratory syncytial virus infection in adults aged 65 years and older, as well as in younger adults with high-risk conditions.
Design and population
The study was an educational activity designed to provide participants with knowledge about respiratory syncytial virus infection in adults aged 65 years and older, as well as in younger adults with high-risk conditions.
Core methods
The methods involved an educational activity where participants were provided with information on the epidemiology, clinical presentation, outcomes, diagnostic challenges, current treatment options, and emerging treatment and prevention strategies for respiratory syncytial virus infection in the specified adult populations.
Main results
The results indicated that participants were able to describe the epidemiology of respiratory syncytial virus infection, discuss its clinical presentation and outcomes in adults aged 65 years and older and in younger adults with high-risk conditions, and address diagnostic challenges, current treatment options, and new treatment and prevention options.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating the effectiveness of the educational activity in enhancing participants' understanding of respiratory syncytial virus infection in vulnerable adult populations, highlighting the novelty of the approach in addressing knowledge gaps in this area.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of targeted educational programs for healthcare professionals to improve the recognition and management of respiratory syncytial virus infection in older adults and younger adults with high-risk conditions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its educational design, which does not provide empirical data on clinical outcomes or treatment efficacy. The evidence is based on an educational activity, which may not fully capture the complexities of real-world clinical scenarios.

使用出生日历时间作为准随机变异来源探索 呼吸道合胞病毒 与哮喘之间的关系

Exploring the Relationship Between Respiratory Syncytial Virus and Asthma Using Calendar Time at Birth as a Source of Quasi‐Random Variation
作者： Michelle C. Dimitris, Daniel R. Feikin, Erin Sparrow, Susan E. Schultz, Jeffrey C. Kwong, Sarah A. Buchan, David A. Savitz, Steven M. Brunwasser, Deshayne B. Fell
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
观察性研究表明严重的 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 与哮喘之间存在关联,但尚不清楚这种关系是因果关系还是混杂关系。
设计与对象
本研究利用出生时间作为准随机变异来源,针对严重RSV感染的个体风险因素进行调查。研究分析了加拿大安大略省2002–2013年间出生的1,437,731名婴儿的全省范围链接行政数据。
核心方法
我们使用回归模型来研究以下之间的关系:(i) RSV、流感和轮状病毒住院治疗与小儿哮喘 发病率 最高的周内的婴儿年龄;(ii) 每周 发病率 RSV、流感和轮状病毒住院情况以及小儿哮喘的概率。
主要结果
We observed the highest odds of asthma among infants aged 13–16 weeks during the RSV peak, 11–13 weeks during the influenza peak, and 16–19 weeks during the rotavirus peak. The relationship between weekly incidence of RSV hospitalisation and probability of paediatric asthma was small in magnitude; we estimated a 3.15 (95% confidence interval −0.07, 6.36) percentage‐point change in probability of paediatric asthma per 1 percentage‐point change in weekly incidence of RSV hospitalisation among infants.
解释与创新
我们发现支持有限因果作用的证据并不能完全解释 RSV 与哮喘之间的关系。其他机制,包括季节性因素,可能会导致之前报道的关联。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,季节性变化可能在 RSV 和哮喘之间的关联中发挥作用,这可以为未来研究病毒感染与哮喘发展之间的联系机制提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到观察设计和潜在混杂因素的限制,但由于使用了大型管理数据集和回归模型,证据充足。

Exploring the Relationship Between Respiratory Syncytial Virus and Asthma Using Calendar Time at Birth as a Source of Quasi‐Random Variation

Authors: Michelle C. Dimitris, Daniel R. Feikin, Erin Sparrow, Susan E. Schultz, Jeffrey C. Kwong, Sarah A. Buchan, David A. Savitz, Steven M. Brunwasser, Deshayne B. Fell
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background Observational studies suggest an association between severe respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and asthma, but it is unclear if this relationship is causal or confounded. Objectives We investigated this relationship using calendar time at birth as a source of quasi‐random variation with respect to individual‐level risk factors for severe RSV infection. We similarly examined influenza (another respiratory virus) and rotavirus (a negative control exposure). Methods We analysed province‐wide linked administrative data for 1,437,731 infants born in Ontario, Canada, from 2002–2013 (which facilitated 5 years of follow‐up for asthma). We ascertained whether each infant experienced an RSV, influenza, and/or rotavirus hospitalisation (and the weekly incidences of RSV, influenza, and rotavirus hospitalisation among infants), and whether each child had asthma at 5 years of age. We used regression models to investigate the relationships between: (i) infant ages during the weeks with the highest incidences of RSV, influenza, and rotavirus hospitalisation and paediatric asthma; (ii) weekly incidences of RSV, influenza, and rotavirus hospitalisation and probability of paediatric asthma. Results We observed the highest odds of asthma among infants aged 13–16 weeks during the RSV peak, 11–13 weeks during the influenza peak, and 16–19 weeks during the rotavirus peak. The relationship between weekly incidence of RSV hospitalisation and probability of paediatric asthma was small in magnitude; we estimated a 3.15 (95% confidence interval −0.07, 6.36) percentage‐point change in probability of paediatric asthma per 1 percentage‐point change in weekly incidence of RSV hospitalisation among infants (offset by 13 weeks, which was the infant age at RSV peak that conferred maximum crude odds of paediatric asthma). Additionally, an unexpected relationship between rotavirus seasonal variation and asthma emerged. Conclusions We found evidence supporting a limited causal role that did not fully explain the relationship between RSV and asthma. Other mechanisms, including seasonal factors, may contribute to previously reported associations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Observational studies suggest an association between severe respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and asthma, but it is unclear if this relationship is causal or confounded.
Design and population
We investigated this relationship using calendar time at birth as a source of quasi‐random variation with respect to individual‐level risk factors for severe RSV infection. We analysed province‐wide linked administrative data for 1,437,731 infants born in Ontario, Canada, from 2002–2013.
Core methods
We used regression models to investigate the relationships between: (i) infant ages during the weeks with the highest incidences of RSV, influenza, and rotavirus hospitalisation and paediatric asthma; (ii) weekly incidences of RSV, influenza, and rotavirus hospitalisation and probability of paediatric asthma.
Main results
We observed the highest odds of asthma among infants aged 13–16 weeks during the RSV peak, 11–13 weeks during the influenza peak, and 16–19 weeks during the rotavirus peak. The relationship between weekly incidence of RSV hospitalisation and probability of paediatric asthma was small in magnitude; we estimated a 3.15 (95% confidence interval −0.07, 6.36) percentage‐point change in probability of paediatric asthma per 1 percentage‐point change in weekly incidence of RSV hospitalisation among infants.
Interpretation and novelty
We found evidence supporting a limited causal role that did not fully explain the relationship between RSV and asthma. Other mechanisms, including seasonal factors, may contribute to previously reported associations.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that seasonal variation may play a role in the association between RSV and asthma, which could inform future studies on the mechanisms linking viral infections to asthma development.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational design and potential confounding factors, but the evidence is strong due to the use of large administrative datasets and regression models.

Journal: Research Square  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-20  |  DOI: 10.21203/rs.3.rs-10461500/v1  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026

呼吸道合胞病毒 住院儿童重症肺炎的病原谱和危险因素的联合检测:一项基于下一代测序的靶向研究

Co-detection pathogen spectrum and risk factors for severe pneumonia in hospitalized children with respiratory syncytial virus: a targeted next-generation sequencing-based study
作者： Yanyan He, Shuangshuang Chen, Hong Wang, Bin Yang, Yuting Yang, Huanyu Liu, Qun Xiao, Tianbi Yang, Hao Zhang, Zhixu Ye
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是儿童下呼吸道感染 (LRTI) 的主要原因。众所周知,年龄、早产和潜在合并症等因素与 RSV 的严重程度有关,但同时检测到的病原体的存在是否与其相关仍不清楚。
设计与对象
该研究纳入了2024年8月至2025年12月在贵州省上海儿童医学中心贵州医院因LRTI住院并通过靶向二代测序(tNGS)检测RSV阳性的5岁以下儿童的临床数据。根据疾病严重程度,将儿童分为重症组(n=173)和非重症组(n=79)。
核心方法
使用单变量和多变量逻辑回归分析来确定重症肺炎的独立危险因素,结果以比值比(OR)和95%置信区间(CI)报告。
主要结果
A total of 252 cases, 152 boys (60.3%) were admitted with RSV-positive LRTI. Severe disease was documented for 173 cases (68.7%). Patients with severe disease were younger than those with non-severe disease (median age, 7.8 months (IQR, 3.6~17.0 months) vs. 17 months (IQR, 8.8~26.5 months; P < 0.001). Staphylococcus aureus (OR = 3.25, 95% CI: 1.23~8.55, p = 0.017) and Pneumocystis jirovecii (OR = 2.59, 95% CI: 1.24~5.41, p = 0.011) were associated with severe disease, while RSV co-infected with other viral pathogens (OR = 0.30, 95% CI: 0.13~0.69, p = 0.005) and higher birth weight (OR = 0.52, 95% CI: 0.32~0.85, p = 0.008) showed a protective effect against severe RSV disease.
解释与创新
这项 队列研究 研究揭示了 RSV 阳性肺炎儿童中共同检测到的病原体的多样性,并确定了与严重疾病相关的特定风险和保护因素。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持针对所有幼儿(包括早产儿)的预防策略,对特定高危人群中 5 岁或以下的儿童具有潜在益处。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对临床数据的依赖和潜在的混杂因素而受到限制,但由于使用了有针对性的下一代测序和统计分析,证据很充分。

Co-detection pathogen spectrum and risk factors for severe pneumonia in hospitalized children with respiratory syncytial virus: a targeted next-generation sequencing-based study

Authors: Yanyan He, Shuangshuang Chen, Hong Wang, Bin Yang, Yuting Yang, Huanyu Liu, Qun Xiao, Tianbi Yang, Hao Zhang, Zhixu Ye
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background: Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) is the primary cause of lower respiratory tract infections(LRTI ) in children. It is well-known that factors such as age, prematurity, and underlying comorbidities are associated with the severity of RSV, but whether the presence of co-detected pathogens is related to it remains unclear. In this study, we aimed to use targeted next-generation sequencing (tNGS) to describe the spectrum of co-detected pathogens in children with RSV-positive pneumonia admitted for LRTI, and risk factors for severe pneumoniain in children. Methods: This study included clinical data from children under 5 years of age who were hospitalized for LRTI and tested positive for RSV by tNGS between August 2024 and December 2025 at Guizhou Hospital, Shanghai Children's Medical Center, Guizhou Province.The children were divided into severe groups (n=173)and non-severe groups (n=79)based on disease severity. Univariate and multivariate logistic regression analyses were used to identify independent risk factors for severe pneumonia, and results were reported as odds ratios (OR) and 95% confidence intervals (CI). Results: A total of 252 cases,152 boys(60.3%)were admitted with RSV-positive LRTI .Severe disease was documented for 173 cases (68.7%).Patients with severe disease were younger than those with non severe disease(median age, 7.8 months(IQR, 3.6~17.0 months)vs. 17 months(IQR, 8.8~26.5 months; P S. aureus (OR = 3.25, 95% CI: 1.23~8.55, p = 0.017) and P. jirovecii (OR = 2.59, 95% CI: 1.24~5.41, p = 0.011) were associated with severe disease, while RSVco-infected with other viral (OR = 0.30, 95% CI: 0.13~0.69, p = 0.005) and higher birth weight (OR = 0.52, 95% CI: 0.32~0.85, p = 0.008) showed a protective effect against severe RSV disease. Conclusion: In this cohort study reveal the diversity of co-detected pathogens in children with RSV-positive pneumonia. We identified that CHD, S. jirovecii increase the risk of severe illness, while RSV co-infected with other viral and higher birth weight have a protective effect. These findings support prevention strategies for all younger children, including premature infants, with potential benefit for children aged 5years or younger in specific high-risk groups.

Question and background
Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) is the primary cause of lower respiratory tract infections (LRTI) in children. It is well-known that factors such as age, prematurity, and underlying comorbidities are associated with the severity of RSV, but whether the presence of co-detected pathogens is related to it remains unclear.
Design and population
This study included clinical data from children under 5 years of age who were hospitalized for LRTI and tested positive for RSV by targeted next-generation sequencing (tNGS) between August 2024 and December 2025 at Guizhou Hospital, Shanghai Children's Medical Center, Guizhou Province. The children were divided into severe groups (n=173) and non-severe groups (n=79) based on disease severity.
Core methods
Univariate and multivariate logistic regression analyses were used to identify independent risk factors for severe pneumonia, and results were reported as odds ratios (OR) and 95% confidence intervals (CI).
Main results
A total of 252 cases, 152 boys (60.3%) were admitted with RSV-positive LRTI. Severe disease was documented for 173 cases (68.7%). Patients with severe disease were younger than those with non-severe disease (median age, 7.8 months (IQR, 3.6~17.0 months) vs. 17 months (IQR, 8.8~26.5 months; P < 0.001). Interpretation and novelty
This cohort study reveals the diversity of co-detected pathogens in children with RSV-positive pneumonia and identifies specific risk and protective factors associated with severe disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support prevention strategies for all younger children, including premature infants, with potential benefit for children aged 5 years or younger in specific high-risk groups.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on clinical data and the potential for confounding factors, but the evidence is strong due to the use of targeted next-generation sequencing and statistical analyses.

槐生物碱的抗人呼吸道合胞病毒活性评价及脂质体气管内给药增强功效的评价

An evaluation of the anti-human respiratory syncytial virus activity of sophora alkaloids and the enhanced efficacy by liposomes via intratracheal administration
作者： Guikang Wang, Ke Zhang, Lei Huang, Chunyuan Liu, Shaoju Hu, Yusi Luo, Zipeng Gong, Jianqing Peng
摘要中文翻译人类 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿、幼儿和老年人严重下呼吸道感染和相关死亡的主要原因。由于 RSV 的高 突变 发生率以及现有 抗病毒药物 药物的疗效和安全性有限,RSV 的临床管理具有挑战性。苦参生物碱是从苦参中提取的天然化合物,具有广谱 抗病毒药物 和免疫调节活性。从机制上讲,RSV依赖ERK1/2磷酸化来促进病毒复制和传播,而槐花生物碱通过调节MEK/ERK信号通路抑制ERK1/2磷酸化,从而抑制病毒增殖和RSV感染引起的炎症反应。体内研究表明,槐花生物碱通过气管内给药可显着降低 RSV 感染小鼠的 死亡率。为了进一步增强药物的肺滞留,采用脂质体来提高苦参生物碱的治疗效果。槐果碱(SC)作为抗RSV感染最有效的生物碱,将其封装在脂质体中以增强其抗RSV感染的效果。体内研究表明,在 RSV 感染的小鼠模型中,负载 SC 的脂质体在抑制 RSV 复制和减轻肺部炎症方面优于游离 SC。本研究不仅阐明了槐花生物碱抗RSV感染的机制,而且提供了SC的脂质体制剂,以提高抗RSV的治疗效果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人类 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿、幼儿和老年人严重下呼吸道感染和相关死亡的主要原因。由于 RSV 的高 突变 发生率以及现有 抗病毒药物 药物的疗效和安全性有限,RSV 的临床管理具有挑战性。
设计与对象
使用 RSV 感染的小鼠模型进行体内研究,以评估槐树生物碱及其脂质体制剂的抗 RSV 活性。
核心方法
槐生物碱,特别是槐果碱 (SC),被封装在脂质体中以增强肺滞留和治疗效果。作用机制涉及通过调节 MEK/ERK 信号通路抑制 ERK1/2 磷酸化。
主要结果
体内研究表明,槐花生物碱通过气管内给药可显着降低 RSV 感染小鼠的 死亡率。在小鼠模型中,负载 SC 的脂质体在抑制 RSV 复制和减轻肺部炎症方面优于游离 SC。
解释与创新
该研究阐明,槐花生物碱通过抑制 ERK1/2 磷酸化、抑制病毒增殖和炎症反应来抵抗 RSV 感染。SC的脂质体制剂为提高抗RSV治疗效果提供了一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,脂质体槐果碱可能是 RSV 感染的一种有前途的治疗策略,有可能改善婴儿、幼儿和老年人等高危人群的 治疗 结局。
局限与证据强度
该证据基于体内小鼠模型,可能无法完全转化为人类临床结果。证据的强度是中等的,因为该研究在动物模型中证明了有效性,但需要在人体试验中进一步验证。

An evaluation of the anti-human respiratory syncytial virus activity of sophora alkaloids and the enhanced efficacy by liposomes via intratracheal administration

Authors: Guikang Wang, Ke Zhang, Lei Huang, Chunyuan Liu, Shaoju Hu, Yusi Luo, Zipeng Gong, Jianqing Peng
Original abstract (English)
Human respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a major cause of severe lower respiratory tract infections and related deaths in infants, young children, and the elderly. The clinical management of RSV is challenging due to its high mutation rate and the limited efficacy and safety of existing antiviral drugs. Sophora alkaloids, natural compounds derived from Sophora flavescens, exhibit broad-spectrum antiviral and immunomodulatory activities. Mechanistically, RSV relies on ERK1/2 phosphorylation to facilitate viral replication and spread, while sophora alkaloids inhibit ERK1/2 phosphorylation via regulating MEK/ERK signaling pathway, thereby suppressing viral proliferation and the inflammatory response caused by RSV infection. In vivo studies demonstrated that sophora alkaloids significantly reduced the mortality of RSV-infected mice via intratracheal administration. To further enhance the pulmonary retention of drugs, liposomes were used to improve the therapeutic effects of sophora alkaloids. As the most effective sophora alkaloids against RSV infection, sophocarpine (SC) was encapsulated in liposomes to enhance its anti-RSV infection effect. In vivo studies demonstrate that SC-loaded liposomes outperformed the free SC in suppressing RSV replication and alleviating pulmonary inflammation in murine models of RSV infection. This study not only clarifies the mechanism by which sophora alkaloids counteract RSV-infection but also provides liposomal formulation of SC to improve the anti-RSV therapeutic effects.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a major cause of severe lower respiratory tract infections and related deaths in infants, young children, and the elderly. The clinical management of RSV is challenging due to its high mutation rate and the limited efficacy and safety of existing antiviral drugs.
Design and population
In vivo studies were conducted using murine models of RSV infection to evaluate the anti-RSV activity of sophora alkaloids and their liposomal formulations.
Core methods
Sophora alkaloids, particularly sophocarpine (SC), were encapsulated in liposomes to enhance pulmonary retention and therapeutic effects. The mechanism of action involved inhibition of ERK1/2 phosphorylation via regulation of the MEK/ERK signaling pathway.
Main results
In vivo studies demonstrated that sophora alkaloids significantly reduced the mortality of RSV-infected mice via intratracheal administration. SC-loaded liposomes outperformed free SC in suppressing RSV replication and alleviating pulmonary inflammation in murine models.
Interpretation and novelty
The study clarifies that sophora alkaloids counteract RSV infection by inhibiting ERK1/2 phosphorylation and suppressing viral proliferation and inflammatory response. The liposomal formulation of SC provides a novel approach to improve anti-RSV therapeutic effects.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that liposomal sophocarpine could be a promising therapeutic strategy for RSV infection, potentially improving treatment outcomes in high-risk populations such as infants, young children, and the elderly.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on in vivo murine models, which may not fully translate to human clinical outcomes. The strength of the evidence is moderate, as the study demonstrates efficacy in animal models but requires further validation in human trials.

低收入国家三级护理中心收治的 RSV 和非 RSV 毛细支气管炎 的比较研究

A Comparative Study on RSV and Non-RSV Bronchiolitis Admitted in A Tertiary Care Center in a Low-Income Country
作者： Kamrun Nahar, Ayesha Begum, Asma Ferdousi, Syeda Humaida Hasan, Tania Sultana, Mitra Datta, Fahmida Faiza Binty
摘要中文翻译背景:呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是引起 毛细支气管炎 的最常见微生物。RSV 和非 RSV 毛细支气管炎 的比较评估对于指导临床管理、优化资源利用和为预防策略提供信息至关重要。材料和方法:2022年10月至2023年3月,这项前瞻性研究在吉大港医学院医院儿科进行。通过预先设计的调查问卷记录了92名两岁以下儿童的人口特征和临床特征。平均值和标准差用于表示人口统计数据。卡方分析用于检查组比例的差异。结果:92例中RSV抗原阳性率为45.7%,平均年龄4.18±3.08个月。抗原阳性病例中,重症毛细支气管炎病例更为常见(22.8%),且住院时间最长。11 月(42.4%)和 12 月(29.3%)期间,RSV 感染水平达到峰值。这项研究描述,在季节性流行期间,RSV 是主要问题,因为在接下来的几个月里,阳性病例数量有所减少。RSV阳性和阴性病例的体征和症状没有显着差异。结论:在 毛细支气管炎 儿童中,RSV 和非 RSV 感染的临床表现和疾病严重程度没有差异,但 RSV 是 毛细支气管炎 JCMCTA 2026 流行的主要贡献者;37 (1):84-90
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是引起 毛细支气管炎 的最常见微生物。RSV 和非 RSV 毛细支气管炎 的比较评估对于指导临床管理、优化资源利用和为预防策略提供信息至关重要。
设计与对象
2022年10月至2023年3月,这项前瞻性研究在吉大港医学院医院儿科进行。该研究包括 92 名两岁以下的儿童。
核心方法
通过预先设计的问卷记录儿童的人口统计学特征和临床特征。平均值和标准差用于表示人口统计数据。卡方分析用于检查组比例的差异。
主要结果
92例RSV抗原阳性率为45.7%,平均年龄4.18±3.08个月。抗原阳性病例中,重症毛细支气管炎病例更为常见(22.8%),且住院时间最长。11 月(42.4%)和 12 月(29.3%)期间,RSV 感染水平达到峰值。这项研究描述,在季节性流行期间,RSV 是主要问题,因为在接下来的几个月里,阳性病例数量有所减少。
解释与创新
RSV阳性和阴性病例的体征和症状没有显着差异。在 毛细支气管炎 儿童中,RSV 和非 RSV 感染的临床表现和疾病严重程度没有差异,但 RSV 是 毛细支气管炎 流行的主要因素。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,RSV 是低收入国家 毛细支气管炎 暴发 的重要贡献者,凸显了在季节性高峰期间采取有针对性的 监测 和预防策略的必要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在一个三级护理中心进行的,这可能会限制普遍性。证据是温和的,因为它基于观察数据,没有控制混杂变量。

A Comparative Study on RSV and Non-RSV Bronchiolitis Admitted in A Tertiary Care Center in a Low-Income Country

Authors: Kamrun Nahar, Ayesha Begum, Asma Ferdousi, Syeda Humaida Hasan, Tania Sultana, Mitra Datta, Fahmida Faiza Binty
Original abstract (English)
Background: Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) is the most common organism causing bronchiolitis. Comparative evaluation of RSV and non-RSV bronchiolitis is essential to guide clinical management, optimize resource utilization, and inform preventive strategies. Materials and methods: From October 2022 to March 2023, this prospective study was conducted in the Pediatrics Department of Chittagong Medical College Hospital, Chattogram. The demographic characteristics and clinical features of Ninety-two children younger than two years were noted by predesigned questionnaire. The mean and standard deviation were used to express the demographic data. Chi-square analysis was used to examine differences in group proportions. Results: RSV antigen positivity was detected in 45.7% of the 92 cases, with a mean age of 4.18±3.08 months. Severe bronchiolitis cases were more common (22.8%) among antigen positive cases and had the longest hospital stay. During the months of November (42.4%) and December (29.3%), RSV infection levels peaked. This study describes that during the seasonal epidemics, RSV was the main concern, as the number of positive cases decreased in the months that followed. The signs and symptoms of RSV positive and negative cases were not significantly different. Conclusion: In children with bronchiolitis, clinical presentation and severity of disease didn’t differ between RSV and non-RSV infections but RSV was the main contributor to epidemic outbreak JCMCTA 2026 ; 37 (1) : 84-90
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) is the most common organism causing bronchiolitis. Comparative evaluation of RSV and non-RSV bronchiolitis is essential to guide clinical management, optimize resource utilization, and inform preventive strategies.
Design and population
From October 2022 to March 2023, this prospective study was conducted in the Pediatrics Department of Chittagong Medical College Hospital, Chattogram. Ninety-two children younger than two years were included in the study.
Core methods
The demographic characteristics and clinical features of the children were noted by predesigned questionnaire. The mean and standard deviation were used to express the demographic data. Chi-square analysis was used to examine differences in group proportions.
Main results
RSV antigen positivity was detected in 45.7% of the 92 cases, with a mean age of 4.18±3.08 months. Severe bronchiolitis cases were more common (22.8%) among antigen positive cases and had the longest hospital stay. During the months of November (42.4%) and December (29.3%), RSV infection levels peaked. This study describes that during the seasonal epidemics, RSV was the main concern, as the number of positive cases decreased in the months that followed.
Interpretation and novelty
The signs and symptoms of RSV positive and negative cases were not significantly different. In children with bronchiolitis, clinical presentation and severity of disease didn’t differ between RSV and non-RSV infections but RSV was the main contributor to epidemic outbreak.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that RSV is a significant contributor to bronchiolitis outbreaks in low-income countries, highlighting the need for targeted surveillance and preventive strategies during seasonal peaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in a single tertiary care center, which may limit generalizability. The evidence is moderate as it is based on observational data without controlling for confounding variables.

呼吸道合胞病毒 和甲型流感住院患者的合并症危险因素和 呼吸道合胞病毒:一项回顾性观察 队列研究 研究

Comorbidity Risk Factors and Mortality in Patients Hospitalized with Respiratory Syncytial Virus and Influenza A: a Retrospective Observational Cohort Study
作者： Christoffer Vigen, Bo Langhoff Hønge, Alexander Dutschke, Jacob Redder, Thomas Greve, Lars Skov Dalgaard, Anita Rath Sørensen, Lotte Ebdrup, Rajesh Mohey, Britta Tarp
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在比较因实验室确诊的 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和甲型流感而住院的成人中的 死亡率,并确定合并症作为死亡的危险因素。
设计与对象
一项回顾性 队列研究 研究的对象是 2019 年 2 月至 2024 年 2 月期间入住丹麦中部地区一家医院且 RSV 或甲型流感检测呈阳性的成年人。
核心方法
结果为住院、30 天、90 天和 180 天 死亡率。使用包括年龄、性别和合并症的 Cox 回归。
主要结果
队列研究共纳入4,416名患者,其中1,366例为RSV感染,3,050例为甲型流感。在所有随访期内,RSV患者的死亡率均高于甲型流感患者。调整后的风险比(aHR)在所有随访期内,RSV患者的死亡率也均高于甲型流感患者。
解释与创新
作者解释说,在住院成人中,RSV 与 死亡率 的相关性高于甲型流感,这强调了合并症作为危险因素的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持针对老年人和患有合并症的患者采取有针对性的预防策略,这可以为公共卫生措施和临床实践提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究是回顾性和观察性的,这可能会引入偏差并限制因果推理的强度。然而,由于样本量大和统计调整,证据是强有力的。

Comorbidity Risk Factors and Mortality in Patients Hospitalized with Respiratory Syncytial Virus and Influenza A: a Retrospective Observational Cohort Study

Authors: Christoffer Vigen, Bo Langhoff Hønge, Alexander Dutschke, Jacob Redder, Thomas Greve, Lars Skov Dalgaard, Anita Rath Sørensen, Lotte Ebdrup, Rajesh Mohey, Britta Tarp
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Purpose: To compare mortality in adults hospitalized with laboratory-confirmed respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) with influenza A and identify comorbidity as risk factors for death. Methods : We performed a retrospective cohort study including adults admitted to a hospital in the Central Denmark Region from February 2019 to February 2024 with a positive RSV or influenza A test. Outcomes were in-hospital, 30-, 90- and 180-day mortality. A cox regression including age, sex and comorbidities was used. Results: The cohort comprised 4,416 patients: 1,366 with RSV and 3,050 with influenza A. Patients with RSV were older (median age 74 vs 67) and more often had at least one comorbidity (76% vs 61%). Mortality was higher for RSV than influenza A across all follow-up periods. Adjusted hazard ratios (aHRs) for mortality were also higher for RSV for all follow up periods. In adjusted 90-day models, shared risk factors for death were chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (COPD) (aHR RSV 1.59, 1.10–2.28; influenza A aHR 1.69, 1.18–2.42) and chronic liver disease (RSV 4.21, 1.54–11.49; influenza A 2.81, 1.23–6.40). In patients with RSV chronic heart disease (aHR 1.46, 1.02–2.09) was also associated with increased risk of death. Asthma showed inverse associations in both models, significant only for RSV (aHR 0.43, 0.19–0.98). Diabetes was not associated with increased risk for any of the patient groups. Conclusions: Among hospitalized adults, RSV was associated with higher mortality than influenza A. Mortality risk factor findings support targeted prevention in older adults and patients with comorbidity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to compare mortality in adults hospitalized with laboratory-confirmed respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and influenza A, and to identify comorbidity as risk factors for death.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study was conducted in adults admitted to a hospital in the Central Denmark Region from February 2019 to February 2024 with a positive RSV or influenza A test.
Core methods
Outcomes were in-hospital, 30-, 90- and 180-day mortality. A cox regression including age, sex and comorbidities was used.
Main results
The cohort comprised 4,416 patients: 1,366 with RSV and 3,050 with influenza A. Mortality was higher for RSV than influenza A across all follow-up periods. Adjusted hazard ratios (aHRs) for mortality were also higher for RSV for all follow up periods.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that RSV was associated with higher mortality than influenza A in hospitalized adults, highlighting the importance of comorbidity as a risk factor.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support targeted prevention strategies in older adults and patients with comorbidities, which could inform public health measures and clinical practices.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is retrospective and observational, which may introduce bias and limit the strength of causal inference. However, the evidence is strong due to the large sample size and statistical adjustments.

阿根廷、巴西和哥伦比亚三所三级医院住院儿童严重 呼吸道合胞病毒 感染的经济和健康负担:一项为期两年的前瞻性多中心 队列研究 研究

Economic and health burden of severe respiratory syncytial virus infection among hospitalised children at three tertiary hospitals in Argentina, Brazil, and Colombia: a two-year prospective multicentre cohort study
作者： Gonzalo G. Guiñazú, Julia Dvorkin, Pilar Sorgente, Pablo Pakciarz, María Pico, Maria Sol Crespi, Ranniery Acuña, Tamara Schvarstein, Luiz Vicente Ribeiro Ferreira da Silva Filho, Alejandro Colmenares
摘要中文翻译背景 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是幼儿严重下呼吸道感染 (LRTI) 的主要原因,但拉丁美洲有关其社会和健康负担的前瞻性数据仍然很少。我们估计了三个拉丁美洲国家中与 RSV 相关的严重 LRTI 相关的直接医疗、直接非医疗和间接费用以及健康负担(伤残调整生命年,DALY),并与因严重 LRTI 住院的 RSV 阴性儿童进行比较。方法 我们于 2023 年 5 月至 2025 年 5 月期间对阿根廷、巴西和哥伦比亚三所三级医院因严重 LRTI 住院的 1-59 个月儿童进行了一项前瞻性多中心 队列研究 研究。RSV 通过逆转录聚合酶链反应 (RT-PCR) 得到证实。收集了有关医疗保健利用、家庭自付费用和护理人员生产力损失的个人层面数据。成本通过自下而上的微观成本计算进行估算,并以 2025 年 6 月调整后的购买力平价美元 (USD PPP) 表示。按国家、RSV 状态和护理水平总结了每次发作的伤残调整生命年 (DALY)。结果 在 747 名儿童中,368 名 (49%) 呈 RSV 阳性。重症监护病房 (ICU) 入院是费用和伤残调整生命年 (DALY) 的主要驱动因素:ICU 每次发作的平均社会总成本比提前入院高出 2 至 6 倍(在阿根廷,RSV 阳性儿童的 PPP 为 22,099 美元,而 RSV 阳性儿童的购买力平价为 3479 美元),中位 DALY 则高出 3 至 5 倍(0.0058 与 0.0011)。不同站点的成本差异很大,但 RSV 状态的差异很小;在所有三个地点,RSV 阳性和 RSV 阴性事件之间的差异很小且不显着(所有 95% 置信区间 [CI] 均包括零)。解释 严重的 RSV 疾病给阿根廷、巴西和哥伦比亚的三所三级医院带来了巨大的经济和健康负担。ICU 入住,而不是 RSV 状态,是每期费用和 DALY 的主要决定因素,为 RSV 预防政策提供了证据。资助默克研究者研究计划 (MISP)。

问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是幼儿严重下呼吸道感染 (LRTI) 的主要原因,但有关拉丁美洲社会和健康负担的前瞻性数据仍然很少。

我们于 2023 年 5 月至 2025 年 5 月期间对阿根廷、巴西和哥伦比亚三所三级医院因严重 LRTI 住院的 1-59 个月儿童进行了一项前瞻性多中心 队列研究 研究。

主要结果
在 747 名儿童中,有 368 名(49%)RSV 呈阳性。入住 ICU 是费用和 DALY 的主要驱动因素:ICU 每次发作的平均社会总成本比未来入院高出 2 到 6 倍(在阿根廷,RSV 阳性儿童的 PPP 为 22,099 美元 vs 3479 美元),DALY 中位数高出 3 到 5 倍(0.0058 比 0.0011)。

Economic and health burden of severe respiratory syncytial virus infection among hospitalised children at three tertiary hospitals in Argentina, Brazil, and Colombia: a two-year prospective multicentre cohort study

Authors: Gonzalo G. Guiñazú, Julia Dvorkin, Pilar Sorgente, Pablo Pakciarz, María Pico, Maria Sol Crespi, Ranniery Acuña, Tamara Schvarstein, Luiz Vicente Ribeiro Ferreira da Silva Filho, Alejandro Colmenares
Original abstract (English)
Background Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of severe lower respiratory tract infection (LRTI) in young children, yet prospective data on its societal and health burden in Latin America remain scarce. We estimated the direct medical, direct non-medical, and indirect costs and the health burden (disability-adjusted life years, DALYs) of severe RSV-associated LRTI, compared with RSV-negative children hospitalised with severe LRTI, in three Latin American countries. Methods We conducted a prospective multicentre cohort study between May 2023 and May 2025 among children aged 1–59 months hospitalised with severe LRTI in three tertiary hospitals in Argentina, Brazil, and Colombia. RSV was confirmed by Reverse Transcription Polymerase Chain Reaction (RT-PCR). Individual-level data on healthcare utilisation, household out-of-pocket expenditures, and caregiver productivity losses were collected. Costs were estimated by bottom-up micro-costing and expressed in June 2025 purchasing power parity–adjusted US dollars (USD PPP). DALYs per episode were summarised by country, RSV status, and level of care. Findings Among 747 children, 368 (49%) were RSV-positive. Intensive care unit (ICU) admission was the main driver of both costs and DALYs: mean total societal costs per episode were two-to six fold higher for ICU than forward admissions (in Argentina, USD PPP 22,099 vs 3479 among RSV-positive children), and median DALYs were three-to five fold higher (0.0058 vs 0.0011). Costs varied widely across sites but differed minimally by RSV status; differences between RSV-positive and RSV-negative episodes were small and non-significant in all three sites (all 95% confidence intervals [CIs] included zero). Interpretation Severe RSV disease imposes a substantial economic and health burden across the three tertiary hospitals studied in Argentina, Brazil, and Colombia. ICU admission, rather than RSV status, is the main determinant of per-episode costs and DALYs, providing evidence to inform policy on RSV prevention. Funding Merck Investigator Studies Program (MISP).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of severe lower respiratory tract infection (LRTI) in young children, yet prospective data on its societal and health burden in Latin America remain scarce.
Design and population
We conducted a prospective multicentre cohort study between May 2023 and May 2025 among children aged 1–59 months hospitalised with severe LRTI in three tertiary hospitals in Argentina, Brazil, and Colombia.
Core methods
RSV was confirmed by Reverse Transcription Polymerase Chain Reaction (RT-PCR). Individual-level data on healthcare utilisation, household out-of-pocket expenditures, and caregiver productivity losses were collected.
Main results
Among 747 children, 368 (49%) were RSV-positive. ICU admission was the main driver of both costs and DALYs: mean total societal costs per episode were two-to six fold higher for ICU than forward admissions (in Argentina, USD PPP 22,099 vs 3479 among RSV-positive children), and median DALYs were three-to five fold higher (0.0058 vs 0.0011).
Interpretation and novelty
Severe RSV disease imposes a substantial economic and health burden across the three tertiary hospitals studied in Argentina, Brazil, and Colombia.
Scientific and public-health significance
ICU admission, rather than RSV status, is the main determinant of per-episode costs and DALYs, providing evidence to inform policy on RSV prevention.
Limitations and evidence strength
Costs varied widely across sites but differed minimally by RSV status; differences between RSV-positive and RSV-negative episodes were small and non-significant in all three sites (all 95% confidence intervals [CIs] included zero).

婴儿中的 呼吸道合胞病毒 毛细支气管炎:临床特征和结果的决定因素

Respiratory syncytial virus bronchiolitis in infants: Clinical profile and determinants of outcome
作者： Sumitha L, Vinod H. Ratageri, Keerthidarshini Khanappanavar, Mahesh Shankar
摘要中文翻译目标:呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿 毛细支气管炎 和住院的主要原因,对儿科发病率和 死亡率 影响很大。该研究的目的是描述 RSV 毛细支气管炎 婴儿的临床特征和结果,并确定与疾病严重程度、毛细支气管炎 和住院时间相关的因素。材料和方法:这项回顾性观察研究包括 2024 年 12 月至 2025 年 11 月期间因 毛细支气管炎 入院且经逆转录聚合酶链反应证实 RSV 感染的 0-24 个月儿童。对人口统计、临床特征、合并症、调查、治疗和结果的数据进行了分析。结果: 纳入儿童 80 名,中位年龄 3 个月;70% 的儿童年龄小于 6 个月。呼吸窘迫是普遍存在的,97.5% 的人需要呼吸支持。无创通气最常用(46.3%)。中位住院天数为 9 天(四分位数范围 6-15)。死亡率 为 11.3%。免疫不完全 (p = 0.010)、合并症 (p = 0.002) 和血培养阳性 (p = 0.008) 与 死亡率 相关。纯母乳喂养与较短的住院时间相关(p = 0.001)。雾化治疗并没有减少住院时间。结论:RSV 毛细支气管炎 主要影响小婴儿,其结局主要由 毛细支气管炎 相关因素决定。预防策略和优化的支持护理仍然至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿 毛细支气管炎 和住院的主要原因,对儿科发病率和 死亡率 影响很大。该研究的目的是描述 RSV 毛细支气管炎 婴儿的临床特征和结果,并确定与疾病严重程度、毛细支气管炎 和住院时间相关的因素。
设计与对象
这项回顾性观察研究包括 2024 年 12 月至 2025 年 11 月期间因 毛细支气管炎 入院且经逆转录聚合酶链反应证实 RSV 感染的 0-24 个月儿童。对人口统计、临床特征、合并症、调查、管理和结果的数据进行了分析。
核心方法
该研究采用回顾性观察设计,收集了 毛细支气管炎 入院儿童的数据,并通过逆转录聚合酶链反应证实了 RSV 感染。分析包括人口统计、临床和结果数据。
主要结果
包括 80 名儿童,中位年龄为 3 个月;70% 的儿童年龄小于 6 个月。呼吸窘迫是普遍存在的,97.5% 的人需要呼吸支持。无创通气最常用(46.3%)。中位住院天数为 9 天(四分位数范围 6-15)。死亡率 为 11.3%。
解释与创新
作者解释说,RSV 毛细支气管炎 主要影响年幼婴儿,其结果主要由 宿主 相关因素决定。该研究确定了 死亡率 的具体危险因素以及感染 RSV 毛细支气管炎 的婴儿住院时间较短的情况。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明 宿主 相关因素在确定 RSV 毛细支气管炎 婴儿结局方面的重要性,并强调了预防策略和优化支持护理的必要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的选择偏倚而受到限制。证据是中等的,因为它基于观察数据并且不包括随机对照试验。

Respiratory syncytial virus bronchiolitis in infants: Clinical profile and determinants of outcome

Authors: Sumitha L, Vinod H. Ratageri, Keerthidarshini Khanappanavar, Mahesh Shankar
Original abstract (English)
Objectives: Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is the leading cause of bronchiolitis and hospitalisation in infants, contributing significantly to paediatric morbidity and mortality. The objective of the study is to describe the clinical profile and outcomes of infants with RSV bronchiolitis and identify factors associated with disease severity, mortality, and length of hospital stay. Material and Methods: This retrospective observational study included children aged 0–24 months admitted with bronchiolitis and reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction-confirmed RSV infection between December 2024 and November 2025. Data on demographics, clinical features, comorbidities, investigations, management and outcomes were analysed. Results: Eighty children were included, with a median age of 3 months; 70% were younger than 6 months. Respiratory distress was universal, and 97.5% required respiratory support. Non-invasive ventilation was most commonly used (46.3%). Median hospital stay was 9 days (interquartile range 6–15). Mortality was 11.3%. Incomplete immunisation ( p = 0.010), comorbidities ( p = 0.002) and positive blood cultures ( p = 0.008) were associated with mortality. Exclusive breastfeeding was associated with shorter hospital stay ( p = 0.001). Nebulised therapies did not reduce hospitalisation duration. Conclusion: RSV bronchiolitis predominantly affects young infants, with outcomes determined mainly by host-related factors. Preventive strategies and optimised supportive care remain essential.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is the leading cause of bronchiolitis and hospitalisation in infants, contributing significantly to paediatric morbidity and mortality. The objective of the study is to describe the clinical profile and outcomes of infants with RSV bronchiolitis and identify factors associated with disease severity, mortality, and length of hospital stay.
Design and population
This retrospective observational study included children aged 0–24 months admitted with bronchiolitis and reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction-confirmed RSV infection between December 2024 and November 2025. Data on demographics, clinical features, comorbidities, investigations, management and outcomes were analysed.
Core methods
The study was conducted using a retrospective observational design, with data collected from children admitted with bronchiolitis and confirmed RSV infection through reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction. The analysis included demographic, clinical, and outcome data.
Main results
Eighty children were included, with a median age of 3 months; 70% were younger than 6 months. Respiratory distress was universal, and 97.5% required respiratory support. Non-invasive ventilation was most commonly used (46.3%). Median hospital stay was 9 days (interquartile range 6–15). Mortality was 11.3%.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that RSV bronchiolitis predominantly affects young infants, with outcomes determined mainly by host-related factors. The study identifies specific risk factors for mortality and shorter hospital stays in infants with RSV bronchiolitis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the importance of host-related factors in determining outcomes for infants with RSV bronchiolitis and highlight the need for preventive strategies and optimised supportive care.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for selection bias. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on observational data and does not include randomised controlled trials.

2025 年巴西流感和 呼吸道合胞病毒 导致幼儿和老年人的 死亡率 和住院人数大幅增加

Large increase in mortality and hospital admissions among young children and the aged due to Influenza and Respiratory Syncytial Virus in Brazil in 2025
作者： Kupek, E.
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查呼吸道病毒对巴西 死亡率 和住院人数的影响,特别强调 2025 年第二季度幼儿和老年人负担增加。背景表明,巴西因严重急性呼吸道感染 (SARI) 导致的 死亡率 和住院人数在 2025 年 1 月至 8 月期间达到峰值。
设计与对象
该研究使用了巴西卫生部关于 SARI 造成的住院和死亡数据,按年龄组(<5、5-14、15-49、50-64、65+ 岁)和 2023 年 1 月至 2025 年 6 月期间的季度编制。这些数据被汇总为 SARS-Cov-2、流感、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和其他病毒(副流感、腺病毒、鼻病毒、博卡病毒、偏肺病毒)。
核心方法
当这些未经实验室证实的死亡原因时,使用多项回归来估算可能的死亡原因。入院率的变化很大程度上遵循死亡率的变化。
主要结果
In the second quarter of 2025 (2025/2), RSV mortality rate among children <5 years reached 60 per 100,000 - a 43% increase compared with 2024/2. The mortality rate for the joint impact of parainfluenza, adenovirus, rhinovirus, bocavirus, and metapneumovirus in the same age group doubled from 20 to 40 on the same scale in 2025/2 compared to 2024/2. Over the same period, influenza mortality tripled among the aged, whereas mortality due to other respiratory viruses increased less dramatically, except for SARS-CoV-2, which decreased among the aged from 150 to 25 per 100,000 between 2023/1 and 2025/2.
解释与创新
作者解释说,虽然自 2023 年以来 SARS-CoV-2 造成的死亡和住院人数迅速下降,但其他呼吸道病毒(主要是流感和 RSV)的 死亡率 发病率在 2025 年 2 月在 5 岁以下儿童和老年人中显着增加。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,公共卫生政策应优先考虑 疫苗 对抗流感和 RSV 的情况,以缓解 2025 年巴西幼儿和老年人中观察到的 死亡率 和住院人数的增加。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于汇总数据,并依靠多项回归来估算未经实验室确认的死亡原因,这可能会带来一些不确定性。证据来自国家 监测 系统,报告结果具有特定的数值变化,支持适度的置信度评估。

Large increase in mortality and hospital admissions among young children and the aged due to Influenza and Respiratory Syncytial Virus in Brazil in 2025

Authors: Kupek, E.
Original abstract (English)
BackgroundMortality and hospital admissions due to Severe Acute Respiratory Infection (SARI) peaked between January and August 2025 in Brazil. MethodsThe Brazilian Ministry of Health data on hospital admissions and deaths caused by SARI were compiled by age group (<5, 5-14, 15-49, 50-64, 65+ years) and quarter between January 2023 and June 2025. SARI causes were aggregated into SARS-Cov-2, Influenza, Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV), and other viruses (parainfluenza, adenovirus, rhinovirus, bocavirus, metapneumovirus). Multinomial regression was used to impute likely causes of death when these were not laboratory confirmed. ResultsIn the second quarter of 2025 (2025/2), RSV mortality rate among children <5 years reached 60 per 100,000 - a 43% increase compared with 2024/2. Mortality rate for the joint impact of parainfluenza, adenovirus, rhinovirus, bocavirus, and metapneumovirus in the same age group doubled from 20 to 40 on the same scale in 2025/2 compared to 2024/2. Over the same period, influenza mortality tripled among the aged, whereas mortality due to other respiratory viruses increased less dramatically, except for SARS-CoV-2, which decreased among the aged from 150 to 25 per 100,000 between 2023/1 and 2025/2. Other age groups remained relatively stable over the period. The variation in hospital admissions largely followed that of mortality. ConclusionsWhile deaths and hospital admissions caused by SARS-CoV-2 declined rapidly since 2023, mortality rates of other respiratory viruses, mainly influenza and RSV, increased significantly among children <5 years and the aged in 2025/2. Public health policies that facilitate vaccine uptake against these infections should be given high priority.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the impact of respiratory viruses on mortality and hospital admissions in Brazil, particularly highlighting the increased burden observed in young children and the aged during the second quarter of 2025. The background indicates that mortality and hospital admissions due to Severe Acute Respiratory Infection (SARI) peaked between January and August 2025 in Brazil.
Design and population
The study used Brazilian Ministry of Health data on hospital admissions and deaths caused by SARI, compiled by age group (<5, 5-14, 15-49, 50-64, 65+ years) and quarter between January 2023 and June 2025. The data were aggregated into SARS-Cov-2, Influenza, Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV), and other viruses (parainfluenza, adenovirus, rhinovirus, bocavirus, metapneumovirus).
Core methods
Multinomial regression was used to impute likely causes of death when these were not laboratory confirmed. The variation in hospital admissions largely followed that of mortality.
Main results
In the second quarter of 2025 (2025/2), RSV mortality rate among children <5 years reached 60 per 100,000 - a 43% increase compared with 2024/2. The mortality rate for the joint impact of parainfluenza, adenovirus, rhinovirus, bocavirus, and metapneumovirus in the same age group doubled from 20 to 40 on the same scale in 2025/2 compared to 2024/2. Over the same period, influenza mortality tripled among the aged, whereas mortality due to other respiratory viruses increased less dramatically, except for SARS-CoV-2, which decreased among the aged from 150 to 25 per 100,000 between 2023/1 and 2025/2.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that while deaths and hospital admissions caused by SARS-CoV-2 declined rapidly since 2023, mortality rates of other respiratory viruses, mainly influenza and RSV, increased significantly among children <5 years and the aged in 2025/2.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that public health policies should prioritize vaccine uptake against influenza and RSV to mitigate the increased mortality and hospital admissions observed in young children and the aged in Brazil during 2025.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on aggregated data and relies on multinomial regression to impute causes of death when not laboratory confirmed, which may introduce some uncertainty. The evidence is derived from a national surveillance system, and the results are reported with specific numerical changes, supporting a moderate confidence assessment.

IDSA 2025 关于使用 疫苗 预防免疫功能低下患者季节性 RSV 感染的指南。

IDSA 2025 Guidelines on the Use of Vaccines for the Prevention of Seasonal RSV Infections in Immunocompromised Patients.
作者： Chen Sabrina Tan, Shweta Anjan, Ella J Ariza-Heredia, Francisco Magana, Timothy D Minniear, Dipleen Kaur, Yngve Falck-Ytter, Lindsey Baden
摘要中文翻译免疫功能低下的个体因 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染而遭受不成比例的严重后果,但指导该人群 呼吸道合胞病毒 的直接证据仍然有限。为了支持 2025-2026 年呼吸道病毒季节的临床和共同决策,美国传染病学会 (IDSA) 召集了一个多学科专家小组,针对免疫功能低下的成人和儿童中的 RSV 疫苗接种 制定快速、基于证据的建议。该小组对 2024 年 8 月至 2025 年 7 月期间发布的比较有效性和危害数据进行了系统审查,并辅以 疫苗 诚信项目的其他证据。使用 GRADE 方法评估证据的确定性和推荐强度。两项针对免疫功能低下成人的测试阴性病例对照研究表明,疫苗接种 将 RSV 相关住院率减少了 70%(95% CI:66%-73%)。来自老年人群的间接证据表明,针对危重疾病的有效性为 81%(95% CI:52%-92%)。在三项随机试验中,接种疫苗组和未接种疫苗组之间的严重不良事件具有可比性。吉兰-巴利综合征很少见,估计每百万剂中就有 11 例超额病例。鉴于严重疾病的大幅减少和严重伤害的可能性较低,专家组强烈建议针对免疫力受损的成人和青少年使用适合年龄的 RSV 疫苗接种。对于 18 岁以下的免疫功能低下患者,建议共同决策。时间应按亚组个体化,考虑 治疗 周期、移植状态和 B 细胞消耗疗法。家庭成员应在符合条件时保持最新状态,并且可以接受与流感和冠状病毒病 2019 疫苗 联合用药。研究重点包括保护的相关性、免疫力的持久性、亚组安全性以及加强剂量的作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
免疫功能低下的个体因 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染而遭受不成比例的严重后果,但指导该人群 呼吸道合胞病毒 的直接证据仍然有限。
设计与对象
为了支持 2025-2026 年呼吸道病毒季节的临床和共同决策,美国传染病学会 (IDSA) 召集了一个多学科专家小组,针对免疫功能低下的成人和儿童中的 RSV 疫苗接种 制定快速、基于证据的建议。
核心方法
该小组对 2024 年 8 月至 2025 年 7 月期间发布的比较有效性和危害数据进行了系统审查,并辅以 疫苗 诚信项目的其他证据。使用 GRADE 方法评估证据的确定性和推荐强度。

两项针对免疫功能低下成人的测试阴性病例对照研究表明,疫苗接种 将 RSV 相关住院率减少了 70%(95% CI:66%-73%)。来自老年人群的间接证据表明,针对危重疾病的有效性为 81%(95% CI:52%-92%)。

鉴于严重疾病的大幅减少和严重伤害的可能性较低,专家组强烈建议针对免疫力受损的成人和青少年使用适合年龄的 RSV 疫苗接种。

研究结果支持在免疫功能低下的成人和儿童中实施 RSV 疫苗接种,以减少严重的疾病结果,对临床实践和共同决策具有影响。

在三项随机试验中,接种疫苗组和未接种疫苗组之间的严重不良事件具有可比性。使用 GRADE 方法评估证据的确定性,专家组根据该评估提出了强烈建议。

IDSA 2025 Guidelines on the Use of Vaccines for the Prevention of Seasonal RSV Infections in Immunocompromised Patients.

Authors: Chen Sabrina Tan, Shweta Anjan, Ella J Ariza-Heredia, Francisco Magana, Timothy D Minniear, Dipleen Kaur, Yngve Falck-Ytter, Lindsey Baden
Original abstract (English)
Immunocompromised individuals experience disproportionately severe outcomes from respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection, yet direct evidence to guide vaccination in this population remains limited. To support clinical and shared decision-making for the 2025-2026 respiratory virus season, the Infectious Diseases Society of America (IDSA) convened a multidisciplinary expert panel to develop rapid, evidence-based recommendations on RSV vaccination among immunocompromised adults and children. The panel conducted a systematic review of comparative effectiveness and harms data published between August 2024 and July 2025, supplemented by additional evidence from the Vaccine Integrity Project. Certainty of evidence and recommendation strength were assessed using the GRADE approach. Two test-negative case-control studies in immunocompromised adults showed vaccination reduced RSV-associated hospitalization by 70% (95% CI: 66%-73%). Indirect evidence from older-adult populations demonstrated 81% effectiveness (95% CI: 52%-92%) against critical illness. Across three randomized trials, serious adverse events were comparable between vaccinated and unvaccinated groups. Guillain-Barré syndrome was rare, with an estimated 11 excess cases per million doses. Given substantial reduction in severe disease and low likelihood of serious harm, the panel issued a strong recommendation for age-appropriate RSV vaccination in adults and adolescents with compromised immunity. For immunocompromised patients <18 years, shared decision-making is advised. Timing should be individualized by subgroups, considering treatment cycles, transplant status, and B-cell-depleting therapies. Household members should remain up to date when eligible, and coadministration with influenza and coronavirus disease 2019 vaccines is acceptable. Research priorities include correlates of protection, durability of immunity, subgroup safety, and the role of booster doses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Immunocompromised individuals experience disproportionately severe outcomes from respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection, yet direct evidence to guide vaccination in this population remains limited.
Design and population
To support clinical and shared decision-making for the 2025-2026 respiratory virus season, the Infectious Diseases Society of America (IDSA) convened a multidisciplinary expert panel to develop rapid, evidence-based recommendations on RSV vaccination among immunocompromised adults and children.
Core methods
The panel conducted a systematic review of comparative effectiveness and harms data published between August 2024 and July 2025, supplemented by additional evidence from the Vaccine Integrity Project. Certainty of evidence and recommendation strength were assessed using the GRADE approach.
Main results
Two test-negative case-control studies in immunocompromised adults showed vaccination reduced RSV-associated hospitalization by 70% (95% CI: 66%-73%). Indirect evidence from older-adult populations demonstrated 81% effectiveness (95% CI: 52%-92%) against critical illness.
Interpretation and novelty
Given substantial reduction in severe disease and low likelihood of serious harm, the panel issued a strong recommendation for age-appropriate RSV vaccination in adults and adolescents with compromised immunity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the implementation of RSV vaccination in immunocompromised adults and children to reduce severe disease outcomes, with implications for clinical practice and shared decision-making.
Limitations and evidence strength
Across three randomized trials, serious adverse events were comparable between vaccinated and unvaccinated groups. The certainty of evidence was assessed using the GRADE approach, and the panel issued a strong recommendation based on this evaluation.

IDSA 2025 关于使用 疫苗 预防免疫功能低下患者季节性 COVID-19、流感和 RSV 感染的指南。

IDSA 2025 Guidelines on the Use of Vaccines for the Prevention of Seasonal COVID-19, Influenza, and RSV Infections in Immunocompromised Patients.
作者： Anoma Nellore, Paul Goepfert, Chen Sabrina Tan, Kristina Bajema, Katherine Belden, Dean Blumberg, Morgan J Katz, Daniel Kaul, Tanvi Sharma, Shweta Anjan
摘要中文翻译呼吸道病毒,包括严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2(SARS-CoV-2 或 COVID-19)、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和流感,对免疫功能低下患者构成重大风险,这些患者的 疫苗 反应减弱,发病率较高。为了应对 2025-2026 年季节不断变化的 疫苗 建议,美国传染病协会 (IDSA) 与 疫苗 完整性项目 (VIP) 和合作伙伴组织合作,为美国许可的针对这些病毒的 疫苗 制定了快速指南。该指南适用于因血液系统恶性肿瘤、实体器官或造血细胞移植、使用免疫抑制剂引起的自身免疫性疾病、严重免疫抑制的人类免疫缺陷病毒及类似情况导致免疫力受损的成人和儿童。强烈建议,在中等确定性证据的支持下,支持及时施用适合年龄的 COVID-19、RSV 和流感 疫苗,并指导相对于免疫抑制治疗和移植的最佳时机。这些 疫苗 的共同给药被认为是适当的。在免疫原性、耐久性和临床有效性方面仍然存在研究差距,特别是对于接受 B 细胞消耗疗法或移植后早期的患者。优先领域包括定义保护相关性、优化 疫苗 时间表、评估高剂量或佐剂制剂以及改善实际有效性和安全性数据。公平和获取战略对于确保弱势群体的接受至关重要。这些指南旨在支持基于证据的决策,并强调需要进行协调一致的多病毒研究,以便为免疫功能低下个体制定量身定制的 疫苗接种 策略提供信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
免疫功能低下的患者面临着呼吸道病毒(包括 RSV)的更高风险,疫苗 反应减弱,发病率增加。该研究解决了针对这一弱势群体量身定制的更新的、基于证据的 疫苗接种 指南的需求。
设计与对象
美国传染病协会 (IDSA) 与 疫苗 诚信项目 (VIP) 和合作伙伴组织合作,为美国许可的 疫苗 针对免疫功能低下个体的 RSV、COVID-19 和流感制定了快速指南。
核心方法
指南综合依靠证据审查和共识方法来制定 疫苗 给药时机、共同给药和患者亚组(包括接受免疫抑制治疗或移植后的患者亚组)的建议。
主要结果
由中等确定性证据支持的强烈建议,支持及时施用适合年龄的 RSV 疫苗,并指导相对于免疫抑制治疗和移植的最佳时机。RSV 疫苗 与 COVID-19 和流感 疫苗 共同给药被认为是适当的。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为支持协调的多病毒 疫苗接种 策略,以减轻免疫功能低下患者的风险。新颖之处在于针对 2025-2026 年季节的 RSV、COVID-19 和流感量身定制的快速指南制定。
科研与公卫意义
这些指南为免疫功能低下患者中 RSV 疫苗接种 的循证决策提供依据,强调及时给药和联合给药策略以降低发病率。他们还强调了特定疗法的免疫原性和有效性方面的优先研究差距。
局限与证据强度
由于依赖共识且某些亚组的高质量数据有限,例如接受 B 细胞耗竭治疗或早期移植后的患者,证据强度中等。主要限制包括保护和现实世界有效性数据之间的相关性差距。

IDSA 2025 Guidelines on the Use of Vaccines for the Prevention of Seasonal COVID-19, Influenza, and RSV Infections in Immunocompromised Patients.

Authors: Anoma Nellore, Paul Goepfert, Chen Sabrina Tan, Kristina Bajema, Katherine Belden, Dean Blumberg, Morgan J Katz, Daniel Kaul, Tanvi Sharma, Shweta Anjan
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory viruses-including severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2 or COVID-19), respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), and influenza-pose significant risks to immunocompromised patients, who experience attenuated vaccine responses and higher morbidity. To address evolving vaccine recommendations for the 2025-2026 season, the Infectious Diseases Society of America (IDSA), in collaboration with the Vaccine Integrity Project (VIP) and partner organizations, developed rapid guidelines for US-licensed vaccines targeting these viruses. The guideline applies to adults and children with compromised immunity due to hematologic malignancy, solid organ or hematopoietic cell transplantation, autoimmune disease on immunosuppressants, human immunodeficienty virus with severe immunosuppression, and similar conditions. Strong recommendations, supported by moderate certainty evidence, endorse timely administration of age-appropriate COVID-19, RSV, and influenza vaccines, with guidance on optimal timing relative to immunosuppressive therapy and transplantation. Coadministration of these vaccines is considered appropriate. Research gaps remain in immunogenicity, durability, and clinical effectiveness, particularly for patients receiving B-cell-depleting therapies or early post-transplant. Priority areas include defining correlates of protection, optimizing vaccine schedules, evaluating high-dose or adjuvanted formulations, and improving real-world effectiveness and safety data. Equity and access strategies are essential to ensure uptake among vulnerable populations. These guidelines aim to support evidence-based decision-making and highlight the need for harmonized, multi-virus research to inform tailored vaccination strategies for immunocompromised individuals.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Immunocompromised patients face heightened risks from respiratory viruses including RSV, with attenuated vaccine responses and increased morbidity. The study addresses the need for updated, evidence-based vaccination guidance tailored to this vulnerable population.
Design and population
The Infectious Diseases Society of America (IDSA), in collaboration with the Vaccine Integrity Project (VIP) and partner organizations, developed rapid guidelines for US-licensed vaccines targeting RSV, COVID-19, and influenza in immunocompromised individuals.
Core methods
The guideline synthesis relied on evidence review and consensus methods to formulate recommendations for vaccine administration timing, coadministration, and patient subgroups, including those on immunosuppressive therapies or post-transplant.
Main results
Strong recommendations, supported by moderate certainty evidence, endorse timely administration of age-appropriate RSV vaccines, with guidance on optimal timing relative to immunosuppressive therapy and transplantation. Coadministration of RSV vaccines with COVID-19 and influenza vaccines is considered appropriate.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as supporting harmonized, multi-virus vaccination strategies to mitigate risks in immunocompromised patients. Novelty lies in the tailored, rapid guideline development addressing RSV alongside COVID-19 and influenza for the 2025-2026 season.
Scientific and public-health significance
These guidelines inform evidence-based decision-making for RSV vaccination in immunocompromised patients, emphasizing timely administration and coadministration strategies to reduce morbidity. They also highlight priority research gaps in immunogenicity and effectiveness for specific therapies.
Limitations and evidence strength
Evidence strength is moderate due to reliance on consensus and limited high-certainty data for certain subgroups, such as patients receiving B-cell-depleting therapies or early post-transplant. Key limitations include gaps in correlates of protection and real-world effectiveness data.

对法国不同 RSVpreF 孕产妇 疫苗 策略对预防幼儿 RSV 感染的影响进行健康经济评估:EPIPACT

A health-economic assessment of the impact of different RSVpreF maternal vaccine strategies to prevent RSV infections among young infants, in France: EPIPACT
作者： Yasmine Fahfouhi, Stéphane Fiévez, Robert Cohen, Corinne Levy, Marta C. Nunes, Louis Chillotti, Romain Moreau, William Greenwood, Emmanuelle Blanc, Amy Law
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过评估不同孕产妇 呼吸道合胞病毒 策略的影响,解决 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 对儿科人群,特别是 1 岁以下婴儿的重大临床和经济负担。
设计与对象
法国使用三状态马尔可夫模型进行了一项健康经济评估,重点关注一年内 627,499 名不同出生的人口,根据母亲 疫苗接种 状态分为两个 队列研究。
核心方法
开发了三态马尔可夫模型来模拟替代 RSVpreF 疫苗接种 策略对住院治疗的影响,结果以每次住院避免的成本和疫苗接种所需的数量 (NNV) 形式呈现。
主要结果
Without vaccination, 38,625 RSV-related hospitalizations were estimated annually, costing €120.0 million. The recommended strategy prevented 7,620 hospitalizations, while the extended and year-round strategies avoided 4,111 and 1,306 additional hospitalizations, respectively.
解释与创新
该研究表明,更广泛的 疫苗接种 策略,例如延长和全年的方法,可以在成本增加有限的情况下显着减少 RSV 住院率,突显了这些策略对公共卫生的潜在影响。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为法国公共卫生决策者提供了宝贵的见解,支持考虑扩展 疫苗接种 策略,以最小的额外成本减少 RSV 医院负担。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于模拟人群,不包括现实世界的数据,这可能会限制结果的普遍性。由于依赖于模拟而不是经验数据,证据被认为是中等的。

A health-economic assessment of the impact of different RSVpreF maternal vaccine strategies to prevent RSV infections among young infants, in France: EPIPACT

Authors: Yasmine Fahfouhi, Stéphane Fiévez, Robert Cohen, Corinne Levy, Marta C. Nunes, Louis Chillotti, Romain Moreau, William Greenwood, Emmanuelle Blanc, Amy Law
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: Most of the clinical and economic burden of the respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in pediatric populations is concentrated among infants aged <1 year. Since 2024, RSVpreF vaccine is available in France for the vaccination of pregnant women between 32 and 36 weeks of gestational age, from September to January. This study aimed to model the impact of alternative RSVpreF national vaccination strategies on RSV hospital burden. METHODS: A three-state Markov model - Susceptible to be infected, RSV-related hospitalization, and Death - was developed to assess the number of hospitalizations avoided by alternative vaccination strategies. The model population of 627,499 unique births in one year was split in two cohorts of infants born to vaccinated and unvaccinated women. Four strategies were modeled: no vaccination, vaccination between September and January (recommended), between May and January (extended), and year-round. Results were presented at a population level using a healthcare perspective, as cost per hospitalization avoided, and number needed to vaccinate (NNV). RESULTS: In the absence of vaccination, 38,625 RSV-related hospitalization among infants under 1 year of age were estimated annually, costing €120.0 million. Compared to no vaccination, the recommended strategy prevented 7,620 (-19.7%) RSV-related hospitalizations, 90.4% of them involved infants aged <3 months (99.5% aged <6 months). The related total cost was €126.4 million, corresponding to cost of €844 per hospitalization avoided, and an NNV of 21 vaccinations to avoid one hospitalization. Compared to the recommended strategy, the extended strategy avoided an additional 4,111 (-13.3%) hospitalizations, 42.0% of them involving infants aged <3 months (95.0% aged <6 months). The total cost of this strategy was €134.4 million (+6.3%), corresponding to a cost of €1,929 per additional hospitalization avoided. Total cost of the year-round strategy was €156.2 million, for 1,306 additional hospitalizations avoided. CONCLUSIONS: This is the first cost-effectiveness model to describe the public health impact of alternative vaccination strategies for RSV prevention in France. The number of hospitalizations avoided ranged from 7,600 stays in the recommended strategy, to more than 13,000 in the year-round strategy, corresponding to hospital savings between €24.7 million and €41.5 million. Alternative scenarios were shown to extend prevention to older age groups. Broader vaccination strategies, notably the extended strategy, had a positive impact on RSV hospital burden, with a limited impact on total costs.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the significant clinical and economic burden of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in pediatric populations, particularly among infants under 1 year of age, by evaluating the impact of different maternal vaccination strategies.
Design and population
A health-economic assessment was conducted in France using a three-state Markov model, focusing on a population of 627,499 unique births over one year, split into two cohorts based on maternal vaccination status.
Core methods
A three-state Markov model was developed to simulate the impact of alternative RSVpreF vaccination strategies on hospitalizations, with results presented as cost per hospitalization avoided and number needed to vaccinate (NNV).
Main results
Without vaccination, 38,625 RSV-related hospitalizations were estimated annually, costing €120.0 million. The recommended strategy prevented 7,620 hospitalizations, while the extended and year-round strategies avoided 4,111 and 1,306 additional hospitalizations, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrated that broader vaccination strategies, such as the extended and year-round approaches, could significantly reduce RSV hospitalizations with a limited increase in costs, highlighting the potential public health impact of these strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide valuable insights for public health decision-makers in France, supporting the consideration of extended vaccination strategies to reduce RSV hospital burden with minimal additional cost.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a modeled population and does not include real-world data, which may limit the generalizability of the results. The evidence is considered moderate due to the reliance on simulation rather than empirical data.

接受生物疗法治疗的哮喘患者呼吸道病毒感染的风险比较

Comparative Risk of Respiratory Virus Infections Among Patients with Asthma Treated with Biologic Therapies
作者： Kuang-Ming Liao, Sheng Chi Huang, I-Ha Lao, Chia-Hwei Kuo, Wan-Hsuan Hsu, Jheng-Yan Wu, Chih-Cheng Lai
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道病毒感染是哮喘患者发病的主要原因之一,而针对2型炎症的生物疗法对呼吸道病毒感染风险的比较效果仍不清楚。
设计与对象
我们利用 TriNetX 美国协作网络进行了一项回顾性 队列研究 研究,研究对象包括新开始使用贝那利珠单抗、奥马珠单抗、杜匹单抗或美泊利单抗的哮喘成人患者。
核心方法
我们使用主动比较器、新用户设计和 1:1 倾向评分匹配,将贝那利珠单抗与每种生物制剂进行比较。主要结局是三年来呼吸道病毒感染(COVID-19、流感、RSV 感染和病毒性肺炎)的综合结果。使用 Cox 比例风险模型估算风险比 (HR)。
主要结果
After matching, 5,663 benralizumab–omalizumab pairs, 7,075 benralizumab–dupilumab pairs, and 6,492 benralizumab–mepolizumab pairs were included. Benralizumab was associated with a higher risk of composite respiratory virus infection than omalizumab (HR 1.49, 95% CI 1.33–1.66), dupilumab (HR 1.24, 95% CI 1.11–1.39), and mepolizumab (HR 1.24, 95% CI 1.12–1.38; all p < 0.001).
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为表明贝那利珠单抗的深度嗜酸性粒细胞耗竭可能会损害 抗病毒药物 宿主 防御,强调了在为呼吸道病毒感染风险增加的患者选择生物治疗时的潜在安全考虑。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能会为呼吸道病毒感染(尤其是 COVID-19 和流感)风险较高的严重哮喘患者选择生物疗法提供参考。
局限与证据强度
回顾性设计和对电子健康记录的依赖可能会引入残留的混杂和错误分类偏差,限制因果推断和对更广泛人群的普遍适用性。

Comparative Risk of Respiratory Virus Infections Among Patients with Asthma Treated with Biologic Therapies

Authors: Kuang-Ming Liao, Sheng Chi Huang, I-Ha Lao, Chia-Hwei Kuo, Wan-Hsuan Hsu, Jheng-Yan Wu, Chih-Cheng Lai
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Respiratory virus infections are a major cause of morbidity in asthma. Although biologic therapies targeting type 2 inflammation are widely used for severe asthma, their comparative effects on respiratory virus infection risk remain unclear. Methods We conducted a retrospective cohort study using the TriNetX US Collaborative Network, including adults with asthma who newly initiated benralizumab, omalizumab, dupilumab, or mepolizumab. Using an active comparator, new-user design with 1:1 propensity score matching, we compared benralizumab with each biologic. The primary outcome was a composite of respiratory virus infections (COVID-19, influenza, RSV infection, and viral pneumonia) over three years. Hazard ratios (HRs) were estimated using Cox proportional hazards models. Results After matching, 5,663 benralizumab–omalizumab pairs, 7,075 benralizumab–dupilumab pairs, and 6,492 benralizumab–mepolizumab pairs were included. Benralizumab was associated with a higher risk of composite respiratory virus infection than omalizumab (HR 1.49, 95% CI 1.33–1.66), dupilumab (HR 1.24, 95% CI 1.11–1.39), and mepolizumab (HR 1.24, 95% CI 1.12–1.38; all p < 0.001). Risks of COVID-19 and influenza were consistently higher with benralizumab across all comparisons. Viral pneumonia risk was higher versus dupilumab (HR 1.59, 95% CI 1.18–2.14) but not versus omalizumab or mepolizumab. RSV infection risk did not differ significantly. Conclusions Benralizumab was associated with a higher risk of respiratory virus infections than omalizumab, dupilumab, and mepolizumab. These findings suggest that profound eosinophil depletion may impair antiviral host defense and should be considered when selecting biologic therapy for patients at increased risk of respiratory viral infections.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory virus infections are a major cause of morbidity in asthma, and the comparative effects of biologic therapies targeting type 2 inflammation on respiratory virus infection risk remain unclear.
Design and population
We conducted a retrospective cohort study using the TriNetX US Collaborative Network, including adults with asthma who newly initiated benralizumab, omalizumab, dupilumab, or mepolizumab.
Core methods
Using an active comparator, new-user design with 1:1 propensity score matching, we compared benralizumab with each biologic. The primary outcome was a composite of respiratory virus infections (COVID-19, influenza, RSV infection, and viral pneumonia) over three years. Hazard ratios (HRs) were estimated using Cox proportional hazards models.
Main results
After matching, 5,663 benralizumab–omalizumab pairs, 7,075 benralizumab–dupilumab pairs, and 6,492 benralizumab–mepolizumab pairs were included. Benralizumab was associated with a higher risk of composite respiratory virus infection than omalizumab (HR 1.49, 95% CI 1.33–1.66), dupilumab (HR 1.24, 95% CI 1.11–1.39), and mepolizumab (HR 1.24, 95% CI 1.12–1.38; all p < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as suggesting that profound eosinophil depletion with benralizumab may impair antiviral host defense, highlighting a potential safety consideration when selecting biologic therapy for patients at increased risk of respiratory viral infections.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform the selection of biologic therapies for patients with severe asthma who are at elevated risk of respiratory viral infections, particularly COVID-19 and influenza.
Limitations and evidence strength
The retrospective design and reliance on electronic health records may introduce residual confounding and misclassification bias, limiting causal inference and generalisability to broader populations.
