尼塞韦单抗介绍后对儿童呼吸道病毒流行病学的影响

Impact on Pediatric Respiratory Virus Epidemiology Following Nirsevimab Introduction
作者： Costanza Di Chiara, Vera Rigamonti, Beatrice Rita Campana, Delia Monopoli, Hajrie Seferi, Alberto Villani, Anna Chiara Vittucci, Livia Antilici, Giulia Brigadoi, Daniele Donà
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估尼塞韦单抗对住院儿童急性呼吸道感染(ARI)整体流行病学的影响,因其对呼吸道合胞病毒相关住院的作用已明确,但其对整体ARI模式的影响仍不清楚。
设计与对象
一项回顾性研究在意大利艾米利亚-罗马涅地区帕尔马的一家三级儿科医院进行,涵盖2022年9月1日至2025年4月30日的3个呼吸道季节,包括因ARI住院且经多重聚合酶链式反应检测出至少一种呼吸道病毒阳性的≤5岁儿童。
核心方法
分析了ARI住院率的时间趋势、病毒分布及RSV特异性感染模式。住院率以艾米利亚-罗马涅地区≤5岁常住人口为背景进行了分析。
主要结果
A total of 241 ARI hospitalizations were included, with 347 viral detections, of which 44.7% were monoinfections and 55.3% were coinfections. The incidence of ARI hospitalizations decreased in the 2024–2025 season compared with the previous 2 seasons (from 14.3 to 2.2 per 100 person-months). The overall distribution of respiratory viruses remained stable over time (P-trend = 0.4736), with RSV and rhinovirus accounting for approximately 35% and 30% of detections, respectively. The proportion of RSV monoinfections remained unchanged, whereas RSV coinfections declined significantly (P-trend = 0.0161).
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,在没有病毒替代证据的情况下,尼塞韦单抗 的引入导致儿童 ARI 住院率下降。RSV 合并感染的选择性减少表明,除了 RSV 相关疾病之外,RSV 预防还可以降低感染的复杂性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,RSV 预防可能对降低儿童呼吸道感染的复杂性产生更广泛的影响,可能为未来的 监测 和干预策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究是回顾性的,基于一家三级儿科医院的住院数据,这可能会限制普遍性。由于研究的观察性质和潜在的混杂因素,证据的强度中等。

Impact on Pediatric Respiratory Virus Epidemiology Following Nirsevimab Introduction

Authors: Costanza Di Chiara, Vera Rigamonti, Beatrice Rita Campana, Delia Monopoli, Hajrie Seferi, Alberto Villani, Anna Chiara Vittucci, Livia Antilici, Giulia Brigadoi, Daniele Donà
Original abstract (English)
Background: Nirsevimab prevents respiratory syncytial virus (RSV)-associated hospitalizations in infants. However, its impact on the broader epidemiology of acute respiratory infections (ARI) in hospitalized children remains unclear. Methods: We conducted a retrospective study of ARI hospitalizations among children aged ≤5 years at a tertiary pediatric hospital in Parma, Emilia Romagna region, Italy, across 3 respiratory seasons (from September 1, 2022 to April 30, 2025), spanning the period before and after nirsevimab introduction (October 2024). All children hospitalized with ARI during the seasonal period (September–April) who tested positive for at least 1 respiratory virus by multiplex polymerase chain reaction were included. We analyzed temporal trends in ARI hospitalization rates, viral distribution and RSV-specific infection patterns. Hospitalization rates were contextualized using the resident population aged ≤5 years in Emilia Romagna. Results: A total of 241 ARI hospitalizations were included, with 347 viral detections, of which 44.7% were monoinfections and 55.3% were coinfections. The incidence of ARI hospitalizations decreased in the 2024–2025 season compared with the previous 2 seasons (from 14.3 to 2.2 per 100 person-months). The overall distribution of respiratory viruses remained stable over time (P -trend = 0.4736), with RSV and rhinovirus accounting for approximately 35% and 30% of detections, respectively. The proportion of RSV monoinfections remained unchanged, whereas RSV coinfections declined significantly ( P -trend = 0.0161). Conclusions: Following nirsevimab introduction, pediatric ARI hospitalizations declined without evidence of viral replacement. The selective reduction in RSV coinfections suggests that RSV prevention may reduce infection complexity in addition to RSV-associated disease. Continued surveillance is needed to assess the long-term epidemiologic impact.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the impact of nirsevimab on the broader epidemiology of acute respiratory infections (ARI) in hospitalized children, as its effect on RSV-associated hospitalizations is well established but its influence on overall ARI patterns remains unclear.
Design and population
A retrospective study was conducted at a tertiary pediatric hospital in Parma, Emilia Romagna region, Italy, across 3 respiratory seasons from September 1, 2022, to April 30, 2025, including children aged ≤5 years hospitalized with ARI who tested positive for at least one respiratory virus by multiplex polymerase chain reaction.
Core methods
Temporal trends in ARI hospitalization rates, viral distribution, and RSV-specific infection patterns were analyzed. Hospitalization rates were contextualized using the resident population aged ≤5 years in Emilia Romagna.
Main results
A total of 241 ARI hospitalizations were included, with 347 viral detections, of which 44.7% were monoinfections and 55.3% were coinfections. The incidence of ARI hospitalizations decreased in the 2024–2025 season compared with the previous 2 seasons (from 14.3 to 2.2 per 100 person-months). The overall distribution of respiratory viruses remained stable over time (P-trend = 0.4736), with RSV and rhinovirus accounting for approximately 35% and 30% of detections, respectively. The proportion of RSV monoinfections remained unchanged, whereas RSV coinfections declined significantly (P-trend = 0.0161).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that the introduction of nirsevimab led to a decline in pediatric ARI hospitalizations without evidence of viral replacement. The selective reduction in RSV coinfections suggests that RSV prevention may reduce infection complexity in addition to RSV-associated disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that RSV prevention could have broader implications for reducing the complexity of respiratory infections in children, potentially informing future strategies for surveillance and intervention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is retrospective and based on hospitalization data from a single tertiary pediatric hospital, which may limit generalizability. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study and potential confounding factors.

RSV 特异性中和抗体的胎盘组织病理学和经胎盘转移 The Silver 研究

Placental Histopathology and Transplacental Transfer of RSV-specific Neutralizing Antibodies The Silver Study
作者： Emily W. E. M. Phijffer, Joukje E. Willemsen, Joanne G. Wildenbeest, Larry J. Anderson, Kitty W. M. Bloemenkamp, Floris Groenendaal, Peter G. J. Nikkels, Louis J. Bont, Phijffer EWEM
摘要中文翻译背景:母体通过胎盘抗体转运获得的被动免疫对于保护婴儿在生命早期免受传染病至关重要。我们检查了胎盘组织学特征是否影响天然 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 特异性中和抗体的经胎盘转移。方法:我们进行了一项前瞻性、观察性、横断面研究。孕妇按1:1:1的纳入比例纳入极度早产儿(24-32周)、中度早产儿(33-36周)和足月儿(37-42周)。分析血清中和 RSV-A 和 RSV-B 抗体的脐带-母体比率 (CMR) 与 16 个胎盘组织学特征的关系。结果:总共 85 对母婴可供分析。婴儿胎龄与 RSV-A 和 RSV-B 特异性抗体的 CMR 显着相关(B = 0.10,P < 0.001)。尽管一些胎盘特征显示与 CMR 存在单变量关联,但多变量模型显示只有胎龄与 CMR 独立相关(B = 0.11,P < 0.001)。结论:我们的研究证实,CMR 在妊娠期间会增加。然而,胎盘组织学的变化可能不会影响经胎盘的 RSV-A 和 RSV-B 抗体转移。我们假设胎盘代偿能力和主动转运机制可能在胎盘抗体转运中发挥关键作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
母体通过胎盘抗体转运获得的被动免疫对于保护婴儿在生命早期免受传染病至关重要。我们检查了胎盘组织学特征是否影响天然 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 特异性中和抗体的经胎盘转移。
设计与对象
我们进行了一项前瞻性、观察性、横断面研究。孕妇按1:1:1的纳入比例纳入极度早产儿(24-32周)、中度早产儿(33-36周)和足月儿(37-42周)。
核心方法
分析血清中和 RSV-A 和 RSV-B 抗体的脐带-母体比率 (CMR) 与 16 个胎盘组织学特征的关系。
主要结果
总共有 85 对母婴可供分析。婴儿胎龄与 RSV-A 和 RSV-B 特异性抗体的 CMR 显着相关(B = 0.10,P < 0.001)。尽管一些胎盘特征显示与 CMR 存在单变量关联,但多变量模型显示只有胎龄与 CMR 独立相关(B = 0.11,P < 0.001)。
解释与创新
我们的研究证实,CMR 在妊娠期间会增加。然而,胎盘组织学的变化可能不会影响经胎盘的 RSV-A 和 RSV-B 抗体转移。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,胎龄是经胎盘 RSV 抗体转移的关键决定因素,这可能为监测和改善新生儿被动免疫的策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究是观察性和横断面性的,这限制了建立因果关系的能力。证据是中等的,因为它是基于样本量有限的单一研究。

Placental Histopathology and Transplacental Transfer of RSV-specific Neutralizing Antibodies The Silver Study

Authors: Emily W. E. M. Phijffer, Joukje E. Willemsen, Joanne G. Wildenbeest, Larry J. Anderson, Kitty W. M. Bloemenkamp, Floris Groenendaal, Peter G. J. Nikkels, Louis J. Bont, Phijffer EWEM
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Maternally acquired passive immunity via transplacental antibody transport is crucial for the protection of infants against infectious diseases in early life. We examined whether placental histological characteristics impact the transplacental transfer of natural respiratory syncytial virus (RSV)-specific neutralizing antibodies. METHODS: We performed a prospective, observational, cross-sectional study. Pregnant women were enrolled with a 1:1:1 inclusion ratio of extremely preterm-born infants (24-32 weeks), moderately preterm-born infants (33-36 weeks) and term-born infants (37-42 weeks). Cord-maternal ratios (CMRs) for serum neutralizing RSV-A and -B antibodies were analyzed in relation to 16 placental histologic characteristics. RESULTS: In total, 85 mother-infant pairs were available for analysis. Infant gestational age was significantly correlated with CMRs for RSV-A and -B specific antibodies (B = 0.10, P < 0.001). Although a few placental characteristics showed a univariate association with CMR, the multivariate model showed that only gestational age was independently related to CMR (B = 0.11, P < 0.001). CONCLUSIONS: Our study confirms that CMRs increase during gestation. However, variations in placental histology may not affect transplacental RSV-A and RSV-B antibody transfer. We hypothesize that the placental compensatory capacity and active transport mechanism may play a key role in transplacental antibody transfer.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Maternally acquired passive immunity via transplacental antibody transport is crucial for the protection of infants against infectious diseases in early life. We examined whether placental histological characteristics impact the transplacental transfer of natural respiratory syncytial virus (RSV)-specific neutralizing antibodies.
Design and population
We performed a prospective, observational, cross-sectional study. Pregnant women were enrolled with a 1:1:1 inclusion ratio of extremely preterm-born infants (24-32 weeks), moderately preterm-born infants (33-36 weeks) and term-born infants (37-42 weeks).
Core methods
Cord-maternal ratios (CMRs) for serum neutralizing RSV-A and -B antibodies were analyzed in relation to 16 placental histologic characteristics.
Main results
In total, 85 mother-infant pairs were available for analysis. Infant gestational age was significantly correlated with CMRs for RSV-A and -B specific antibodies (B = 0.10, P < 0.001). Although a few placental characteristics showed a univariate association with CMR, the multivariate model showed that only gestational age was independently related to CMR (B = 0.11, P < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
Our study confirms that CMRs increase during gestation. However, variations in placental histology may not affect transplacental RSV-A and RSV-B antibody transfer.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that gestational age is a key determinant of transplacental RSV antibody transfer, which may inform strategies for monitoring and improving passive immunity in neonates.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is observational and cross-sectional, which limits the ability to establish causality. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a single study with a limited sample size.

用于检测病毒性呼吸道病原体的优化宏基因组纳米孔测序方法的临床验证。

Clinical validation of an optimised metagenomic nanopore sequencing method for detecting viral respiratory pathogens.
作者： Nicholas D Sanderson, Kate E. Dingle, Katie M V Hopkins, Ali Vaughan, Matthew Colpus, Melody Parker, Elisabeth Vilde Dietz, Jessica Gentry, Anita Justice, Sarah Oakley
摘要中文翻译背景:临床宏基因组学 (CMg) 提供高通量呼吸道病原体检测,其范围比靶向、探针依赖性诊断更广泛。测序成本和高 宿主 生物量的挑战是使用 CMg 在鼻咽拭子等非侵入性样本中进行高通量呼吸道病原体检测的障碍。方法:我们优化了纳米孔测序工作流程,通过背景核酸去除和 SISPA 扩增以及牛津纳米孔技术 (ONT) 测序,采用病原体富集来检测鼻咽拭子中的 RNA 病毒。作为未知病原体检测的先决条件,我们首先根据由常规多重 PCR 检测到的 23 个病原体目标组成的黄金标准得出质量控制 (QC) 标准和诊断阈值。在这项诊断准确性研究中,我们使用 344 个前瞻性收集的上呼吸道样本(提交用于常规测试)验证了该工作流程。结果:使用预定义的 QC 和阳性标准,工作流程相对于 PCR 的敏感性为 51% (95%CI: 45%-57%)(检测到 133/260 个阳性目标)(在超过 20 个金标准检测的病原体中,敏感性为 19%-85%),特异性为 99.8%(95%CI:99.6%-99.9%) (未检测到 3836/3845 个阴性目标)。使用事后优化阈值,灵敏度提高至 58% (159/274),仅考虑 RNA 病原体,灵敏度提高至 61% (159/260),排除鼻病毒/肠道病毒,灵敏度提高至 70% (144/207),排除工作流程后 qPCR Ct 值≥35 的样本,灵敏度提高至 83% (140/169)。多重测序的读取交叉造成了大部分 (7/9) 的假阳性。仅鉴定出 2 种可能的其他病原体(鼻病毒和冠状病毒 OC43)。通过测序成功对 41 种 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)、13 种甲型流感病毒和 10 种鼻病毒/肠道病毒进行了亚型分型。多重纳米孔测序成本为 112 英镑/样本。解释:虽然 CMg 具有巨大的诊断潜力,但这项验证研究表明,当前应用于具有高 宿主 和低病原体生物量的上呼吸道样本病毒检测的宏基因组测序方法存在技术局限性。它对较高病毒载量具有更高的敏感性,这表明确定最合适的用例以最大化其效用和价值的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
临床宏基因组学提供高通量呼吸道病原体检测,其范围比有针对性的、依赖于探针的诊断更广泛。该研究旨在验证用于检测病毒呼吸道病原体的优化宏基因组纳米孔测序方法。
设计与对象
这项诊断准确性研究使用 344 份前瞻性收集的上呼吸道样本(提交用于常规检测)验证了测序工作流程。该研究在临床环境中进行,旨在根据由常规多重 PCR 检测到的 23 个病原体目标组成的金标准来评估该方法的性能。
核心方法
该研究优化了纳米孔测序工作流程,以检测鼻咽拭子中的 RNA 病毒,通过背景核酸去除和 SISPA 扩增来富集病原体,并使用牛津纳米孔技术 (ONT) 测序。质量控制 (QC) 标准和诊断阈值是根据由常规多重 PCR 检测到的 23 个病原体目标组成的金标准得出的。
主要结果
使用预定义的 QC 和阳性标准,工作流程与 PCR 相比的灵敏度为 51% (95%CI: 45%-57%)(检测到 133/260 个阳性目标)(在超过 20 个金标准检测的病原体中,灵敏度为 19%-85%),特异性为 99.8% (95%CI: 99.6%-99.9%)(3836/3845 个阴性目标)未检测到)。
解释与创新
该研究证明了当前宏基因组测序方法应用于高 宿主 和低病原体生物量的上呼吸道样本病毒检测的技术局限性。它对较高病毒载量具有更高的敏感性,凸显了确定最合适的用例以最大化其效用和价值的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为开发更有效的病毒呼吸道病原体检测宏基因组测序方法提供了信息,特别是在需要高通量和广谱检测的临床环境中。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据受到非侵入性样本中高宿主生物量和低病原体生物量的技术挑战的限制,这可能会影响宏基因组测序方法的准确性。由于这些限制,证据强度中等。

Clinical validation of an optimised metagenomic nanopore sequencing method for detecting viral respiratory pathogens.

Authors: Nicholas D Sanderson, Kate E. Dingle, Katie M V Hopkins, Ali Vaughan, Matthew Colpus, Melody Parker, Elisabeth Vilde Dietz, Jessica Gentry, Anita Justice, Sarah Oakley
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Clinical metagenomics (CMg) offers high-throughput respiratory pathogen detection with a wider range than targeted, probe-dependent diagnostics. Sequencing cost and the challenges of high host biomass are barriers to the use of CMg in high-throughput respiratory pathogen detection in non-invasive samples such as nasopharyngeal swabs. METHODS: We optimised a nanopore sequencing workflow to detect RNA viruses in nasopharyngeal swabs, employing pathogen enrichment, by background nucleic acid depletion and SISPA amplification, and Oxford Nanopore Technology (ONT) sequencing. As a pre-requisite for agnostic pathogen detection, we first derived quality control (QC) criteria and diagnostic thresholds against a gold-standard comprising 23 pathogen targets detected by routine multiplex PCR. In this diagnostic accuracy study we validated this workflow using 344 prospectively collected upper respiratory tract samples submitted for routine testing. FINDINGS: Using pre-defined QC and positivity criteria, the workflow's sensitivity versus PCR was 51% (95%CI: 45%-57%) (133/260 positive targets detected) (ranging from 19%-85% across pathogens with >20 gold- standard detections), and specificity 99.8% (95%CI: 99.6%-99.9%) (3836/3845 negative targets not detected). Sensitivity improved to 58% (159/274) using post-hoc optimised thresholds, 61% (159/260) only considering RNA pathogens, 70% (144/207) excluding rhinovirus/enterovirus and 83% (140/169) excluding samples with post workflow qPCR Ct values ≥35. Read crossover from multiplex sequencing contributed most (7/9) false-positives. Only 2 plausible additional pathogens were identified (rhinovirus and coronavirus OC43). 41 respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), 13 influenza A and 10 rhinovirus/enterovirus were successfully sub-typed by sequencing. Multiplexed nanopore sequencing costs were £112/sample. INTERPRETATION: Although CMg has substantial diagnostic potential, this validation study demonstrates the technical limitations of current metagenomic sequencing methods applied to viral detection in upper respiratory tract samples with high host and low pathogen biomass. Its greater sensitivity at higher viral loads demonstrates the importance of identifying the most appropriate use cases to maximise its utility and value.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Clinical metagenomics offers high-throughput respiratory pathogen detection with a wider range than targeted, probe-dependent diagnostics. The study aimed to validate an optimised metagenomic nanopore sequencing method for detecting viral respiratory pathogens.
Design and population
This diagnostic accuracy study validated the sequencing workflow using 344 prospectively collected upper respiratory tract samples submitted for routine testing. The study was conducted in a clinical setting to assess the method's performance against a gold-standard comprising 23 pathogen targets detected by routine multiplex PCR.
Core methods
The study optimised a nanopore sequencing workflow to detect RNA viruses in nasopharyngeal swabs, employing pathogen enrichment through background nucleic acid depletion and SISPA amplification, and using Oxford Nanopore Technology (ONT) sequencing. Quality control (QC) criteria and diagnostic thresholds were derived against a gold-standard comprising 23 pathogen targets detected by routine multiplex PCR.
Main results
Using pre-defined QC and positivity criteria, the workflow's sensitivity versus PCR was 51% (95%CI: 45%-57%) (133/260 positive targets detected) (ranging from 19%-85% across pathogens with >20 gold-standard detections), and specificity 99.8% (95%CI: 99.6%-99.9%) (3836/3845 negative targets not detected).
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrates the technical limitations of current metagenomic sequencing methods applied to viral detection in upper respiratory tract samples with high host and low pathogen biomass. Its greater sensitivity at higher viral loads highlights the importance of identifying the most appropriate use cases to maximise its utility and value.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the development of more effective metagenomic sequencing methods for viral respiratory pathogen detection, particularly in clinical settings where high-throughput and broad-spectrum detection is needed.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is limited by the technical challenges of high host biomass and low pathogen biomass in non-invasive samples, which may affect the accuracy of metagenomic sequencing methods. The evidence strength is moderate due to these limitations.

解读 Sirt2 调节与非经典 Wnt/平面细胞极性依赖性脾连接启动之间的关联:两种 Sirt2 调节剂在小鼠内毒素血症中的比较研究。

Deciphering the association between Sirt2 modulation and non-canonical Wnt/planar cell polarity-dependent splenic junctional priming: a comparative study of two Sirt2 modulators in murine endotoxemia.
作者： Doaa A. Zaky, Lamiaa A. Ahmed, Yasmin S. Mohamed
摘要中文翻译通过保持细胞/连接完整性来增强脾功能是对抗感染的一种被低估的有效策略。目前的工作旨在评估沉默调节蛋白(Sirt)调节对与脂多糖(LPS)诱导的内毒素血症相关的大鼠脾功能障碍的影响。内毒素血症是通过单次 LPS 注射(10 mg/kg;腹腔注射)诱导的,可以单独注射,也可以先注射 2 种 Sirt 调节剂中的一种,注射 3 天;AGK2(41 mg/kg/d;腹腔注射)或白藜芦醇(RSV;40 mg/kg/d;腹腔注射)。结果揭示了 AGK2 介导的 Sirt-2 抑制和无翼同源物 Wnt5a 精炼与同时刺激脾受体酪氨酸激酶(如孤受体 2/Frizzled-4/Dishevelled-2 轴)之间存在关联。这与平面细胞极性流(PCP)成分的增强相一致,即鸟苷-5'-三磷酸结合的Ras同源物家族成员-A/rho相关线圈的蛋白激酶-1/2,这反过来又与脾VE-钙粘蛋白含量和免疫组织化学表达的增加以及粘着斑激酶激活磷酸化的刺激有关。这些事件伴随着脾脏分化 T 细胞计数的增加,以阻止血浆内毒素的增加,并部分抑制由此产生的细胞因子风暴。通过恢复脾脏宏观和微观结构、改善生存和临床结果以及改善终末器官损伤标志物,进一步验证了生化修正。白藜芦醇未能达到如此显着的效果;这一结论可能归因于其 Sirt-2 激活能力阻碍了其抗炎作用,最终导致实验模型中内毒素水平轻度下降。Sirt2选择性抑制可能在通过微调Wnt5a/PCP轴和重建具有扩大血液过滤能力的连接支架来更新脾脏模式方面具有优势,此外,免疫细胞的合成和迁移也提供了一种克服潜在内毒素血症的新方法。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了脾细胞和连接完整性在感染控制中的尚未被研究的作用,并试图评估去乙酰化酶 (Sirt) 的调节是否可以减轻脂多糖 (LPS) 诱导的大鼠内毒素血症期间的脾功能障碍。
设计与对象
一项实验研究通过单次腹膜内注射 LPS (10 mg/kg) 诱导大鼠内毒素血症,并预先给予或不给予两种 Sirt 调节剂——AGK2(41 mg/kg/天)或白藜芦醇(RSV;40 mg/kg/天)——持续三天。
核心方法
在 LPS 攻击前三天腹膜内施用 Sirt 调节剂。进行生化和组织学评估以评估脾结构、免疫细胞分化和信号通路,包括 Wnt5a/平面细胞极性 (PCP) 轴和相关分子标记。
主要结果
AGK2 介导的 Sirt2 抑制与 Wnt5a/PCP 通路的激活、脾 VE-钙粘蛋白含量增加和免疫组织化学表达相关,同时增强 T 细胞分化以及部分抑制细胞因子风暴和内毒素水平。白藜芦醇没有达到类似的效果。
解释与创新
作者认为 Sirt2 抑制通过微调 Wnt5a/PCP 轴在恢复脾连接完整性和免疫功能方面具有优越性,为内毒素血症管理提供了一种新的治疗途径。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,选择性 Sirt2 抑制可能会改善内毒素血症的脾功能和临床结果,可能为未来的感染控制和免疫调节策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到动物模型设计、缺乏随机性以及对单一内毒素血症模型的依赖的限制,这限制了对人类感染的普遍适用性;由于这些限制,证据强度被评估为中等。

Deciphering the association between Sirt2 modulation and non-canonical Wnt/planar cell polarity-dependent splenic junctional priming: a comparative study of two Sirt2 modulators in murine endotoxemia.

Authors: Doaa A. Zaky, Lamiaa A. Ahmed, Yasmin S. Mohamed
Original abstract (English)
Enhancement of the splenic function by preserving its cellular/junctional integrity is an underrated effective strategy in combatting infections. The present work aimed to assess the effect of sirtuin (Sirt) modulation in splenic dysfunction associated with lipopolysaccharide (LPS)-induced endotoxemia in rats. Endotoxemia was induced by a single LPS injection (10 mg/kg; i.p) either solely or preceded by one of 2 Sirt modulators for 3 days; AGK2 (41 mg/kg/d; i.p) or resveratrol (RSV; 40 mg/kg/d; i.p). The results revealed an existing association between AGK2-mediated Sirt-2 inhibition and wingless homolog Wnt5a refining with contemporaneous stimulation of the splenic receptor tyrosine kinase like orphan receptor-2/Frizzled-4/Dishevelled-2 axis. This coincided with enhancement of the planar cell polarity stream (PCP) components, namely, Guanosine-5'-triphosphate-bound Ras homolog family member-A/rho-associated coil-containing protein kinase-1/2, that was in turn linked to augmentation of splenic VE-cadherin content and immunohistochemical expression and instigation of focal adhesion kinase activating phosphorylation. These events were accompanied by enhanced splenic count of differentiated T cells to halt the plasma endotoxin increment with partial suppression of the resultant cytokine storm. The biochemical amendment was further validated by restoration of splenic macro- and microscopic architecture, improved survival and clinical outcomes, and amelioration of end-organ damage markers. Resveratrol failed to achieve such remarkable effects; a conclusion that might be attributed to the hinderance of its anti-inflammatory actions by its Sirt-2 activating capacity, leading to eventual mild endotoxin level decrement in the experimental model. The Sirt2 selective inhibition might be superior in renovating splenic patterning by fine-tuning the Wnt5a/PCP axis and reconstructing junctional scaffolding that functions to expand the blood filtration capacity, besides, immune cells synthesis and migration, presenting a novel approach to conquer the underlying endotoxemia.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the understudied role of splenic cellular and junctional integrity in infection control and sought to evaluate whether sirtuin (Sirt) modulation could mitigate splenic dysfunction during lipopolysaccharide (LPS)-induced endotoxemia in rats.
Design and population
An experimental study induced endotoxemia in rats via a single intraperitoneal LPS injection (10 mg/kg), with or without prior administration of two Sirt modulators—AGK2 (41 mg/kg/day) or resveratrol (RSV; 40 mg/kg/day)—for three days.
Core methods
Sirt modulators were administered intraperitoneally for three days prior to LPS challenge. Biochemical and histological assessments were performed to evaluate splenic architecture, immune cell differentiation, and signaling pathways, including the Wnt5a/planar cell polarity (PCP) axis and related molecular markers.
Main results
AGK2-mediated Sirt2 inhibition was associated with activation of the Wnt5a/PCP pathway, increased splenic VE-cadherin content, and immunohistochemical expression, alongside enhanced T-cell differentiation and partial suppression of cytokine storm and endotoxin levels. Resveratrol did not achieve comparable effects.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted Sirt2 inhibition as superior in restoring splenic junctional integrity and immune function by fine-tuning the Wnt5a/PCP axis, presenting a novel therapeutic avenue for endotoxemia management.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that selective Sirt2 inhibition may improve splenic function and clinical outcomes in endotoxemia, potentially informing future strategies for infection control and immune modulation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its animal model design, lack of randomization, and reliance on a single endotoxemia model, which constrains generalizability to human infections; the evidence strength is assessed as moderate due to these constraints.

印度尼西亚老年人 疫苗 的优先级设置:使用 SMART 疫苗 的多标准决策分析。

Priority setting for older adult vaccines in Indonesia: a multi-criteria decision analysis using SMART vaccines.
作者： Mohammed Alfaqeeh, Neily Zakiyah, Maarten J. Postma, Anas Hamad, Auliya A. Suwantika
摘要中文翻译背景:印度尼西亚的老龄化人口面临着巨大的 疫苗 可预防疾病负担,而 疫苗 的引入决策仍然受到资源有限和缺乏优先框架的限制。本研究采用结构化优先顺序方法,为印度尼西亚老年人确定了 疫苗 的优先顺序。研究设计和方法:使用 疫苗 的战略多属性排名工具进行多标准决策分析。对流感 疫苗 (IV)、肺炎球菌结合物 疫苗 (PCV)、重组带状疱疹 疫苗 (RZV) 和 呼吸道合胞病毒 疫苗 (RSVV) 的 10 个属性进行了评估。利益相关者确认了 疫苗 候选者、验证了标准、分配了权重并对 疫苗 进行了评分。进行单向敏感性分析和探索性代理不确定性分析来评估排名的稳健性。结果:来自 10 个利益相关机构的 17 名代表参加了会议。权重最高的属性是质量调整生命年增加/残疾调整生命年避免(14.44%)、与老年人公共卫生优先事项的相关性(12.78%)、避免过早死亡(11.89%)、净预算影响(11.44%)和成本效益(11.22%)。综合得分排名IV第一(858.25),其次是PCV(809.24)、RSVV(569.39)和RZV(558.03)。所有敏感性和代理不确定性分析的排名均保持不变。结论:IV 是印度尼西亚老年人最优先考虑的 疫苗,其次是 PCV、RSVV 和 RZV。该框架为 疫苗 政策和健康老龄化战略提供了循证基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
印度尼西亚在老年人中面临着巨大的 疫苗 可预防疾病负担,同时引入 疫苗 的资源也有限,因此需要一个结构化的优先顺序框架来指导 疫苗 政策决策。
设计与对象
本研究使用多标准决策分析和 疫苗 战略多属性排名工具来优先考虑印度尼西亚老年人的 疫苗,涉及来自 10 个利益相关机构的 17 名代表。
核心方法
该分析评估了四种 疫苗(流感 疫苗、肺炎球菌结合物 疫苗、重组带状疱疹 疫苗 和 呼吸道合胞病毒 疫苗)的 10 个属性,由利益相关者分配权重并对 疫苗 进行评分。
主要结果
综合评分中,流感 疫苗 排名第一 (858.25),其次是肺炎球菌结合物 疫苗 (809.24)、呼吸道合胞病毒 疫苗 (569.39) 和重组带状疱疹 疫苗 (558.03)。所有敏感性和代理不确定性分析的排名均保持不变。
解释与创新
该框架将流感 疫苗 确定为印度尼西亚老年人的最高优先级 疫苗,为政策决策和健康老龄化战略提供了循证基础。
科研与公卫意义
优先顺序框架可以为印度尼西亚老年人免疫规划的基于证据的 疫苗 政策和资源分配提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究对利益相关者的意见和抽象证据的依赖限制了普遍性,并且缺乏临床或流行病学数据使结论的强度降低到中等。

Priority setting for older adult vaccines in Indonesia: a multi-criteria decision analysis using SMART vaccines.

Authors: Mohammed Alfaqeeh, Neily Zakiyah, Maarten J. Postma, Anas Hamad, Auliya A. Suwantika
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Indonesia's aging population faces substantial vaccine-preventable disease burdens, while vaccine introduction decisions remain constrained by limited resources and the absence of a prioritization framework. This study prioritized vaccines for older adults in Indonesia using a structured prioritization approach. RESEARCH DESIGN AND METHODS: A multi-criteria decision analysis was conducted using the Strategic Multi-Attribute Ranking Tool for Vaccines. Influenza vaccine (IV), pneumococcal conjugate vaccine (PCV), recombinant zoster vaccine (RZV), and respiratory syncytial virus vaccine (RSVV) were assessed across 10 attributes. Stakeholders confirmed vaccine candidates, validated criteria, assigned weights, and scored vaccines. One-way sensitivity analyses and an exploratory proxy uncertainty analysis were performed to assess ranking robustness. RESULTS: Seventeen representatives from ten stakeholder institutions participated. The highest weighted attributes were quality-adjusted life years gained/disability-adjusted life years averted (14.44%), relevance to older adult public health priorities (12.78%), premature deaths averted (11.89%), net budget impact (11.44%), and cost-effectiveness (11.22%). Composite scores ranked IV first (858.25), followed by PCV (809.24), RSVV (569.39), and RZV (558.03). Rankings remained unchanged across all sensitivity and proxy uncertainty analyses. CONCLUSIONS: IV was the highest-priority vaccine for older adults in Indonesia, followed by PCV, RSVV, and RZV. This framework provides an evidence-informed basis for vaccine policy and healthy aging strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Indonesia faces substantial vaccine-preventable disease burdens among older adults alongside constrained resources for vaccine introduction, creating a need for a structured prioritization framework to guide vaccine policy decisions.
Design and population
This study used a multi-criteria decision analysis with the Strategic Multi-Attribute Ranking Tool for Vaccines to prioritize vaccines for older adults in Indonesia, involving 17 representatives from ten stakeholder institutions.
Core methods
The analysis assessed four vaccines—influenza vaccine, pneumococcal conjugate vaccine, recombinant zoster vaccine, and respiratory syncytial virus vaccine—across 10 attributes, with stakeholders assigning weights and scoring vaccines.
Main results
Composite scores ranked influenza vaccine first (858.25), followed by pneumococcal conjugate vaccine (809.24), respiratory syncytial virus vaccine (569.39), and recombinant zoster vaccine (558.03). Rankings remained unchanged across all sensitivity and proxy uncertainty analyses.
Interpretation and novelty
The framework identified influenza vaccine as the highest-priority vaccine for older adults in Indonesia, providing an evidence-informed basis for policy decisions and healthy aging strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The prioritization framework can inform evidence-based vaccine policy and resource allocation for older adult immunization programs in Indonesia.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s reliance on stakeholder input and abstract-level evidence limits generalizability, and the absence of clinical or epidemiological data reduces the strength of conclusions to moderate.

使用症状特征的机器学习来区分常见的呼吸道病原体,以优先考虑诊断测试。

Distinguishing common respiratory pathogens using machine learning of symptom profiles to prioritize diagnostic testing.
作者： Chunyan Xiang, Tingting You, Fei Zhou, Dong Liu, Yeming Wang, Bin Cao
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
常见呼吸道病原体之间的临床症状重叠使诊断优先顺序变得复杂,并且缺乏可以指导检测策略的系统比较。
设计与对象
该研究使用大规模的家庭多重 PCR 测试数据集来描述常见呼吸道病原体的症状特征,并按年龄和性别进行分层。
核心方法
分层聚类将病原体分为基于症状的类别,并训练多层感知器 (MLP) 模型来预测这些聚类级别的类别,并使用 AUC、分类准确性和相关指标评估性能。
主要结果
不同病原体的症状特征基本重叠,RSV 主要以呼吸道症状为特征;MLP模型实现了高AUC(0.80-0.92)和超过75%的特异性,但敏感性较低。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为支持使用症状数据来确定诊断测试的优先顺序,特别是在 暴发 期间或在资源有限的环境中,并确定可能为未来 抗病毒药物 试验设计提供信息的系统症状特征。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现谨慎地为临床和 暴发 环境中优先诊断测试的策略提供了信息,并可能指导 抗病毒药物 试验基于症状的患者报告结果的制定。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据受到病原体之间大量症状重叠和模型敏感性较低的限制,导致可信度评估适中。

Distinguishing common respiratory pathogens using machine learning of symptom profiles to prioritize diagnostic testing.

Authors: Chunyan Xiang, Tingting You, Fei Zhou, Dong Liu, Yeming Wang, Bin Cao
Original abstract (English)
Clinical symptoms are critical for diagnosing and managing upper respiratory tract infections, yet systematic comparisons across common pathogens remain limited. We aimed to characterize symptom signatures across common respiratory pathogens and to develop approaches that support the prioritization of diagnostic testing across pathogens. Using a large-scale, home-based multiplex PCR testing dataset, we characterized symptom profiles across common respiratory pathogens stratified by age and sex. Hierarchical clustering was applied to group pathogens into symptom-based categories, and a multilayer perceptron (MLP) model was trained to predict these cluster-level categories. A post-hoc refinement combined MLP outputs with contemporaneous epidemiological data to further prioritize likely pathogens within predicted categories. Model performance was evaluated using the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC), classification accuracy, and related metrics. Symptom profiles overlapped substantially across pathogens, with differences being more categorical than pathogen-specific: RSV, HCoV, HPIV, and HRV were predominantly characterized by respiratory symptoms, whereas influenza A/B and SARS-CoV-2 showed more pronounced systemic manifestations. The MLP model achieved high AUCs (0.80-0.92, depending on age group) with specificities exceeding 75% but lower sensitivities, reflecting a conservative prediction pattern. Integrating cluster-level predictions with contemporaneous epidemiology improved the prioritization of prevalent pathogens, though performance remained limited for less common ones. When expanding predictions to the top three likely pathogens, the model enabled diagnostic test prioritization with an overall accuracy of 0.83 (95% CI, 0.82-0.84). Our study supports the use of readily available symptom data to inform the prioritization of diagnostic testing, particularly during outbreaks or in resource-limited settings where timely laboratory testing is constrained. In addition, the systematic symptom signatures identified across common respiratory pathogens may inform the development and optimization of symptom-based patient-reported outcomes for future antiviral trials.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Clinical symptom overlap among common respiratory pathogens complicates diagnostic prioritization, and systematic comparisons that could guide testing strategies are lacking.
Design and population
The study used a large-scale, home-based multiplex PCR testing dataset to characterize symptom profiles across common respiratory pathogens, stratified by age and sex.
Core methods
Hierarchical clustering grouped pathogens into symptom-based categories, and a multilayer perceptron (MLP) model was trained to predict these cluster-level categories, with performance evaluated using AUC, classification accuracy, and related metrics.
Main results
Symptom profiles overlapped substantially across pathogens, with RSV predominantly characterized by respiratory symptoms; the MLP model achieved high AUCs (0.80–0.92) and specificities exceeding 75%, but lower sensitivities.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as supporting the use of symptom data to inform diagnostic testing prioritization, particularly during outbreaks or in resource-limited settings, and identify systematic symptom signatures that may inform future antiviral trial design.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings cautiously inform strategies for prioritizing diagnostic testing in clinical and outbreak settings and may guide the development of symptom-based patient-reported outcomes for antiviral trials.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s evidence is limited by substantial symptom overlap across pathogens and lower model sensitivity, resulting in a moderate confidence assessment.

LNP-CpG:发挥mRNA 疫苗的自我辅助作用

LNP-CpG: deploy the self-adjuvant role of mRNA vaccines
作者： Ning Luan, Han Cao, Xiaolong Zhang, Fengmei Yang, Caixia Lu, Yang He, Qianqian Li, Yanwei Bi, Zhanlong He, Shengtao Fan
摘要中文翻译随着 mRNA 疫苗 的快速发展,脂质纳米颗粒 (LNP) 因其卓越的核酸递送效率和内在的自我辅助特性而成为非 mRNA 疫苗 模式的关键载体和佐剂。在这项研究中,我们系统地评估了水痘带状疱疹病毒糖蛋白 E (VZV-gE) 亚基 疫苗 框架内结合 LNP 和 CpG 佐剂的各种制剂策略。我们证明,由 LNP 封装的 CpG (LNP-CpG) 形成的均匀纳米颗粒,当与 gE 抗原简单混合时,与其他封装配置相比,可引发优异的免疫原性。肌内注射两剂量 (LNP-CpG)+gE 方案可显着增强小鼠的体液和细胞 免疫应答,明显优于商业 疫苗 Shingrix(以 1/10 人类剂量施用)。至关重要的是,相同的治疗方案在恒河猴中诱导出与人用全剂量 Shingrix 相当的强大的免疫特征。此外,LNP-CpG 在不同的 疫苗 平台上显示出广谱效用,证明了对呼吸道 (RSV) 和嗜神经 (HSV) 病原体的功效,与多种模式的兼容性,包括亚基 (VZV-gE、RSV-Pre-F)、减毒活 (LA-HSV) 和灭活 (i-HSV) 疫苗;以及 VZV+RSV 组合配方中的多功能实施。总的来说,我们的研究结果将 LNP-CpG 定位为一种多功能、安全、高效的佐剂平台,具有巨大的临床转化潜力,为下一代 疫苗 开发提供了令人信服的范例。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究利用脂质纳米颗粒(LNP)作为载体和佐剂,利用其核酸递送效率和内在的自我辅助特性,解决了增强亚基 疫苗 免疫原性的需求。
设计与对象
该研究系统地评估了 LNP-CpG 制剂与亚基 疫苗 框架中的水痘带状疱疹病毒糖蛋白 E (VZV-gE) 的结合,评估了小鼠和恒河猴的免疫原性。
核心方法
作者将与 gE 抗原混合的 LNP 封装的 CpG (LNP-CpG) 与替代封装配置进行了比较,并针对商业 疫苗 Shingrix 进行了基准响应。
主要结果
在小鼠中,肌肉注射两剂量 (LNP-CpG)+gE 方案显着增强了体液和细胞 免疫应答,其表现优于 1/10 人用剂量的 Shingrix,并在恒河猴中诱导出与人用全剂量 Shingrix 相当的免疫特征。
解释与创新
作者将 LNP-CpG 解释为一种多功能佐剂平台,具有跨多种 疫苗 模式和病原体的广谱效用,强调了其下一代 疫苗 开发的转化潜力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,LNP-CpG 可以提高 疫苗 的功效和灵活性,为增强对包括呼吸道病毒在内的多种病原体的保护提供了一种有前途的方法。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于临床前模型(小鼠和猕猴),并且不报告人类临床试验数据,这限制了直接转化主张和对人类群体的普遍性。

LNP-CpG: deploy the self-adjuvant role of mRNA vaccines

Authors: Ning Luan, Han Cao, Xiaolong Zhang, Fengmei Yang, Caixia Lu, Yang He, Qianqian Li, Yanwei Bi, Zhanlong He, Shengtao Fan
Original abstract (English)
With the rapid advancement of mRNA vaccines, lipid nanoparticles (LNPs) have emerged as pivotal carriers and adjuvants for non-mRNA vaccine modalities, driven by their superior nucleic acid delivery efficiency and intrinsic self-adjuvanting properties. In this study, we systematically evaluated various formulation strategies combining LNPs and the CpG adjuvant within a varicella-zoster virus glycoprotein E (VZV-gE) subunit vaccine framework. We demonstrated that uniform nanoparticles formed by LNP-encapsulated CpG (LNP-CpG), when simply admixed with the gE antigen, elicited superior immunogenicity compared to alternative encapsulation configurations. Intramuscular administration of a two-dose (LNP-CpG)+gE regimen significantly augmented both humoral and cellular immune responses in mice, markedly outperforming the commercial vaccine Shingrix (administered at a 1/10 human dose). Crucially, the identical regimen induced robust, comparable immune profiles to a full human dose of Shingrix in rhesus macaques. Furthermore, LNP-CpG displayed broad-spectrum utility across diverse vaccine platforms, demonstrating efficacy against both respiratory (RSV) and neurotropic (HSV) pathogens, compatibility with multiple modalities, including subunit (VZV-gE, RSV-Pre-F), live-attenuated (LA-HSV), and inactivated (i-HSV) vaccines; and versatile implementation in a combined VZV+RSV formulation. Collectively, our findings position LNP-CpG as a versatile, safe, highly efficacious adjuvant platform with substantial clinical translational potential, offering a compelling paradigm for next-generation vaccine development.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need to enhance immunogenicity of subunit vaccines by leveraging lipid nanoparticles (LNPs) as carriers and adjuvants, building on their nucleic acid delivery efficiency and intrinsic self-adjuvanting properties.
Design and population
The research systematically evaluated LNP-CpG formulations combined with varicella-zoster virus glycoprotein E (VZV-gE) in a subunit vaccine framework, assessing immunogenicity in mice and rhesus macaques.
Core methods
The authors compared LNP-encapsulated CpG (LNP-CpG) admixed with gE antigen against alternative encapsulation configurations and benchmarked responses against the commercial vaccine Shingrix.
Main results
In mice, intramuscular administration of a two-dose (LNP-CpG)+gE regimen significantly augmented both humoral and cellular immune responses, outperforming a 1/10 human dose of Shingrix, and induced comparable immune profiles to a full human dose of Shingrix in rhesus macaques.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret LNP-CpG as a versatile adjuvant platform with broad-spectrum utility across multiple vaccine modalities and pathogens, highlighting its translational potential for next-generation vaccine development.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest LNP-CpG could improve vaccine efficacy and flexibility, offering a promising approach for enhancing protection against diverse pathogens including respiratory viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to preclinical models (mice and macaques) and does not report human clinical trial data, which constrains direct translational claims and generalizability to human populations.

呼吸道 RNA 病毒不可知临床宏基因组工作流程的开发

Development of an Agnostic Clinical Metagenomic Workflow for Respiratory RNA Viruses
作者： Kate E. Dingle, Katie M.V. Hopkins, Nicholas D. Sanderson, Ali Vaughan, Daisy Anderton, Matthew Colpus, Melody Parker, Syeda Anam Fatima, Jessica Gentry, Laura Dunn
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
临床宏基因组学为呼吸道病毒诊断提供了一种不可知的方法,但方法学上的挑战阻碍了其有效性。本研究旨在通过开发呼吸道 RNA 病毒的工作流程来应对这些挑战。
设计与对象
该研究评估了英国牛津大学医院 NHS 基金会信托基金收集的鼻咽拭子中的病毒载量和核酸提取物。这些样品用于测试呼吸道病毒的各种富集和去除方法。
核心方法
工作流程涉及预提取病毒富集技术,例如差速离心、珠磨、核酸酶 治疗 和病毒沉淀。提取RNA并使用特定寡核苷酸引物进行反转录以进行后续扩增。
主要结果
最终的工作流程结合了样品澄清、核酸酶 治疗、病毒沉淀和离心,以优化病毒富集。选择序列独立的单引物扩增,因为它优于多重置换扩增。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为证明了呼吸道 RNA 病毒的强大的不可知工作流程,这可以增强分子诊断。这种方法的新颖之处在于它集成了多种富集方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现对于改善临床环境中呼吸道病毒的检测具有重要意义,有可能提高诊断准确性并为公共卫生 监测 策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,可能缺乏详细的统计分析或长期验证。由于缺乏敏感性和特异性的综合数据,证据强度中等。

Development of an Agnostic Clinical Metagenomic Workflow for Respiratory RNA Viruses

Authors: Kate E. Dingle, Katie M.V. Hopkins, Nicholas D. Sanderson, Ali Vaughan, Daisy Anderton, Matthew Colpus, Melody Parker, Syeda Anam Fatima, Jessica Gentry, Laura Dunn
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Clinical metagenomics has the potential to provide an agnostic approach to respiratory virus diagnostics, but there are substantial methodological challenges. Viral loads vary by orders of magnitude among respiratory samples and nucleic acid extracts are dominated by non-target sequences, particularly for samples from the upper respiratory tract. This necessitates upstream virus enrichment and non-specific amplification approaches adaptable to high-throughput assays. We aimed to develop such a workflow for respiratory RNA viruses. Methods We used nasopharyngeal swabs in viral transport medium remaining after routine microbiological testing of clinical samples at Oxford University Hospital NHS Foundation Trust (UK) was completed. Pre-extraction virus enrichment and non-target nucleic acid depletion methods evaluated included differential centrifugation, bead-beating, nuclease treatment and virus precipitation. RNA was extracted, then reverse transcribed using random N 9 priming to include the oligonucleotide (5’-GATGATAGTAGGGCTTCGTCAC-N 9 -3’) used in subsequent amplification. Two amplification approaches were designed and compared side-by-side: isothermal multiple displacement amplification via Equiphi29 DNA polymerase using circularised template versus PCR-based sequence independent single primer amplification. Sequencing used the Oxford Nanopore Technologies platform. Results Our final optimised workflow combined multiple approaches to virus enrichment; sample clarification (3,200g centrifugation, 30 minutes), nuclease treatment, virus precipitation and centrifugation (13,200g, 30 minutes). RNA was extracted from the resultant pellet. Sequence independent single primer amplification was adopted in the final workflow as it consistently out-performed multiple displacement amplification. During development work, we detected a wide range of respiratory viruses, including coronaviruses (SARS-Cov-2, OC43, NC63, 229E), influenza A and B, respiratory syncytial virus, parainfluenza 2, 3 and 4, human metapneumovirus, human rhino- and enteroviruses, together with an extraction control (MS2) and controls for reverse transcription and amplification controls (Zika, murine respirovirus and orthoreovirus RNA). Conclusions We have developed an agnostic clinical metagenomics workflow for RNA respiratory viruses, with potential for use in molecular diagnostics. Subsequent work will determine sensitivity and specificity relative to performance current standard-of-care diagnostics such as commercial respiratory PCR panels.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Clinical metagenomics offers an agnostic approach to respiratory virus diagnostics, but methodological challenges hinder its effectiveness. This study aimed to address these challenges by developing a workflow for respiratory RNA viruses.
Design and population
The study evaluated viral loads and nucleic acid extracts from nasopharyngeal swabs collected at Oxford University Hospital NHS Foundation Trust (UK). These samples were used to test various enrichment and depletion methods for respiratory viruses.
Core methods
The workflow involved pre-extraction virus enrichment techniques such as differential centrifugation, bead-beating, nuclease treatment, and virus precipitation. RNA was extracted and reverse transcribed using a specific oligonucleotide primer for subsequent amplification.
Main results
The final workflow combined sample clarification, nuclease treatment, virus precipitation, and centrifugation to optimize virus enrichment. Sequence independent single primer amplification was selected as it outperformed multiple displacement amplification.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as demonstrating a robust agnostic workflow for respiratory RNA viruses, which could enhance molecular diagnostics. This approach is novel in its integration of multiple enrichment methods.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings have implications for improving the detection of respiratory viruses in clinical settings, potentially enhancing diagnostic accuracy and informing public health surveillance strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, which may lack detailed statistical analysis or long-term validation. The evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of comprehensive data on sensitivity and specificity.

治疗 和婴儿急性病毒 毛细支气管炎 的临床管理方案

TREATMENT AND CLINICAL MANAGEMENT PROTOCOLS FOR ACUTE VIRAL BRONCHIOLITIS IN INFANTS
作者： Max Dias Lemos, Francisco Yago Assunção Serpa, Agatha Caroline Valanera Teixeira, Giovana Boufet Zanini, Bruna Righi Bisognin, Percílio dos Santos Fernandes Júnior, Taísa Yamaguchi, Layla Monteiro de Andrade, Dayane de Castro Lopes, Maria Eduarda Silva Silton
摘要中文翻译目的:严格回顾有关 治疗 方案和婴儿急性病毒 毛细支气管炎 临床治疗的最新科学证据,强调支持性干预、药物治疗、呼吸支持和预防 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染的策略。方法:使用 PubMed 进行叙述性文献综述,采用与病毒 毛细支气管炎 和治疗干预措施相关的关键词。优先考虑最近针对婴儿急性病毒 毛细支气管炎 的临床和治疗管理的研究,包括系统评价、临床试验和循证指南。结果:分析的证据强调,急性病毒 毛细支气管炎 的治疗仍然主要基于支持性措施,确保足够的氧合、水合和营养。低流量氧疗是低氧血症患者的首选治疗方法,而高流量鼻插管治疗可能会在常规支持不足时减少护理升级的需要。支气管扩张剂、肾上腺素、皮质类固醇和抗生素并未显示出一致的临床益处来证明其常规使用的合理性。雾化高渗盐水可能会适度缩短住院时间,但证据质量低限制了其系统推荐。在预防 RSV 相关疾病方面,帕利珠单抗仍然是严重疾病风险较高的特定婴儿的一种选择,而 尼塞韦单抗 和孕产妇免疫则扩大了可用的预防策略。结论:当代急性病毒 毛细支气管炎 的治疗应优先考虑支持性措施和明智、逐步使用呼吸支持,避免缺乏临床益处的药物干预。尽管最近取得了进展,但在呼吸支持方式的选择和升级方面仍然存在差距。新 RSV 预防策略的纳入扩大了减轻疾病负担的可能性,加强了根据现有最佳证据定期更新临床方案的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在严格审查有关 治疗 方案和婴儿急性病毒 毛细支气管炎 临床管理的最新科学证据,强调支持性干预、药物治疗、呼吸支持和预防 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染的策略。
设计与对象
使用 PubMed 进行叙述性文献综述,重点关注近期针对婴儿急性病毒 毛细支气管炎 的临床和治疗管理的研究,包括系统评价、临床试验和循证指南。
核心方法
优先考虑最近针对婴儿急性病毒 毛细支气管炎 的临床和治疗管理的研究,包括系统评价、临床试验和循证指南。
主要结果
分析的证据强调,急性病毒 毛细支气管炎 的治疗仍然主要基于支持性措施,确保足够的氧合、水合和营养。低流量氧疗是低氧血症患者的首选治疗方法,而高流量鼻插管治疗可能会在常规支持不足时减少护理升级的需要。支气管扩张剂、肾上腺素、皮质类固醇和抗生素并未显示出一致的临床益处来证明其常规使用的合理性。雾化高渗盐水可能会适度缩短住院时间,但证据质量低限制了其系统推荐。
解释与创新
作者解释说,当代急性病毒 毛细支气管炎 的治疗应优先考虑支持性措施和明智、逐步使用呼吸支持,避免缺乏经过证实的临床益处的药物干预。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果谨慎地表明,在管理急性病毒 毛细支气管炎 时需要优先考虑支持性护理和逐步使用呼吸支持,同时还强调了使用新的预防策略(如帕利珠单抗、尼塞韦单抗 和孕产妇免疫)更新临床方案的重要性。
局限与证据强度
由于某些研究结果的确定性较低,尤其是雾化高渗盐水,证据受到限制,并且在呼吸支持方式的选择和升级方面仍然存在差距。

TREATMENT AND CLINICAL MANAGEMENT PROTOCOLS FOR ACUTE VIRAL BRONCHIOLITIS IN INFANTS

Authors: Max Dias Lemos, Francisco Yago Assunção Serpa, Agatha Caroline Valanera Teixeira, Giovana Boufet Zanini, Bruna Righi Bisognin, Percílio dos Santos Fernandes Júnior, Taísa Yamaguchi, Layla Monteiro de Andrade, Dayane de Castro Lopes, Maria Eduarda Silva Silton
Original abstract (English)
Objective: To critically review recent scientific evidence regarding treatment protocols and clinical management of acute viral bronchiolitis in infants, emphasizing supportive interventions, pharmacological therapies, respiratory support, and strategies for preventing respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection. Methods: A narrative literature review was conducted using PubMed, employing keywords related to viral bronchiolitis and therapeutic interventions. Priority was given to recent studies addressing the clinical and therapeutic management of acute viral bronchiolitis in infants, including systematic reviews, clinical trials, and evidence-based guidelines. Results: The analyzed evidence reinforces that the management of acute viral bronchiolitis remains predominantly based on supportive measures, ensuring adequate oxygenation, hydration, and nutrition. Low-flow oxygen therapy is the initial approach for patients with hypoxemia, whereas high-flow nasal cannula therapy may reduce the need for escalation of care when conventional support is insufficient. Bronchodilators, epinephrine, corticosteroids, and antibiotics do not show consistent clinical benefit to justify their routine use. Nebulized hypertonic saline may provide a modest reduction in the length of hospital stay, but the low certainty of the evidence limits its systematic recommendation. Regarding the prevention of RSV-associated disease, palivizumab remains an option for selected infants at higher risk of severe disease, while nirsevimab and maternal immunization expand the available prevention strategies. Conclusion: Contemporary management of acute viral bronchiolitis should prioritize supportive measures and the judicious, stepped use of respiratory support, avoiding pharmacological interventions that lack proven clinical benefit. Despite recent advances, gaps remain regarding the selection and escalation of respiratory support modalities. The incorporation of new RSV prevention strategies expands the possibilities for reducing the disease burden, reinforcing the need to periodically update clinical protocols in light of the best available evidence.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to critically review recent scientific evidence regarding treatment protocols and clinical management of acute viral bronchiolitis in infants, emphasizing supportive interventions, pharmacological therapies, respiratory support, and strategies for preventing respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection.

A narrative literature review was conducted using PubMed, focusing on recent studies addressing the clinical and therapeutic management of acute viral bronchiolitis in infants, including systematic reviews, clinical trials, and evidence-based guidelines.
Core methods
Priority was given to recent studies addressing the clinical and therapeutic management of acute viral bronchiolitis in infants, including systematic reviews, clinical trials, and evidence-based guidelines.
Main results
The analyzed evidence reinforces that the management of acute viral bronchiolitis remains predominantly based on supportive measures, ensuring adequate oxygenation, hydration, and nutrition. Low-flow oxygen therapy is the initial approach for patients with hypoxemia, whereas high-flow nasal cannula therapy may reduce the need for escalation of care when conventional support is insufficient. Bronchodilators, epinephrine, corticosteroids, and antibiotics do not show consistent clinical benefit to justify their routine use. Nebulized hypertonic saline may provide a modest reduction in the length of hospital stay, but the low certainty of the evidence limits its systematic recommendation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that contemporary management of acute viral bronchiolitis should prioritize supportive measures and the judicious, stepped use of respiratory support, avoiding pharmacological interventions that lack proven clinical benefit.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings cautiously inform the need to prioritize supportive care and the stepped use of respiratory support in managing acute viral bronchiolitis, while also highlighting the importance of updating clinical protocols with new prevention strategies such as palivizumab, nirsevimab, and maternal immunization.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by the low certainty of some findings, particularly regarding nebulized hypertonic saline, and gaps remain regarding the selection and escalation of respiratory support modalities.

SIRT1 激活剂白藜芦醇可减轻小鼠与年龄相关的皮肤变薄。

SIRT1 Activator Resveratrol Attenuates Age-Related Skin Thinning in Mice.
作者： Fuka Tamura, Iyori Nojima, Risa Kunimoto, Ryusuke Hosoda, Yuko Kuwabara, Keiko Kawakami, Kouhei Horimoto, Atsushi Kuno
摘要中文翻译衰老与表皮和真皮变薄有关,导致皱纹、干燥和皮肤屏障功能受损。Sirtuin-1 (SIRT1) 是一种 NAD+ 依赖性蛋白脱乙酰酶,可抑制细胞衰老和基质金属蛋白酶 (MMP) 表达,这两者都有助于皮肤变薄。在这里,我们研究了膳食白藜芦醇 (RSV)(一种 SIRT1 激活剂)是否可以减轻小鼠与年龄相关的皮肤变薄。与 20 周龄小鼠相比,60 周龄 (wo) 小鼠的表皮和真皮厚度显着减少,并且从 28 周龄开始持续 32 周施用 RSV(0.4 g/kg 饮食)可减弱这些变化。在老年小鼠的表皮中,Ki67 阳性细胞的数量减少,但 RSV 治疗 使 Ki67 阳性细胞的数量恢复。尽管RSV没有改变SIRT1阳性细胞的数量,但它显着减少了乙酰赖氨酸阳性细胞的数量,表明SIRT1活性增强。在真皮中,老年小鼠的 MMP-9 和 MMP-13 免疫阳性区域增加,而在 RSV 治疗的老年小鼠中没有观察到这些与年龄相关的增加。磷酸化 SMAD2 水平随着年龄的增长而降低,但不受 RSV 的影响。此外,人 HaCaT 角质形成细胞中的 SIRT1 敲低会增加 p21 表达和衰老相关的 β-半乳糖苷酶活性。总而言之,这些发现表明 SIRT1 激活剂 RSV 通过抑制表皮细胞衰老和抑制真皮胶原蛋白降解来改善与年龄相关的皮肤变薄。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
衰老与表皮和真皮变薄有关,导致皱纹、干燥和皮肤屏障功能受损。该研究旨在调查膳食白藜芦醇(RSV)(一种 SIRT1 激活剂)是否可以减轻小鼠与年龄相关的皮肤变薄。
设计与对象
该研究以小鼠为对象,测量了 20 只和 60 只小鼠的表皮和真皮厚度。从 28 周龄开始,对 60 只小鼠进行了为期 32 周的 RSV 给药测试。
核心方法
使用组织学分析测量表皮和真皮厚度。通过免疫组织化学评估 Ki67 阳性细胞和乙酰赖氨酸阳性细胞的数量。使用蛋白质印迹评估磷酸化 SMAD2 水平。使用 siRNA 敲低人 HaCaT 角质形成细胞中的 SIRT1,并测量 p21 表达和衰老相关的 β-半乳糖苷酶活性。
主要结果
与 20 周龄小鼠相比,60 周龄小鼠的表皮和真皮厚度显着减少,并且这些变化通过 RSV 给药而减弱。在老年小鼠的表皮中,Ki67 阳性细胞的数量减少,但 RSV 治疗 使 Ki67 阳性细胞的数量恢复。RSV 显着减少了乙酰赖氨酸阳性细胞的数量,表明 SIRT1 活性增强。在真皮中,老年小鼠的 MMP-9 和 MMP-13 免疫阳性区域有所增加,但在 RSV 治疗的老年小鼠中并未观察到这些与年龄相关的增加。磷酸化 SMAD2 水平随着年龄的增长而降低,但不受 RSV 的影响。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,SIRT1 激活剂 RSV 通过抑制表皮细胞衰老和抑制真皮胶原蛋白降解来改善与年龄相关的皮肤变薄。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为未来研究 SIRT1 在与年龄相关的皮肤变化中的作用以及 SIRT1 激活剂在皮肤衰老治疗干预中的潜在用途提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于小鼠,并未考虑人类的变异性。证据基于组织学和生化分析,结果被解释为暗示一种机制,而不是建立因果关系。研究结果的可信度为中等。

SIRT1 Activator Resveratrol Attenuates Age-Related Skin Thinning in Mice.

Authors: Fuka Tamura, Iyori Nojima, Risa Kunimoto, Ryusuke Hosoda, Yuko Kuwabara, Keiko Kawakami, Kouhei Horimoto, Atsushi Kuno
Original abstract (English)
Aging is associated with thinning of the epidermis and dermis, leading to wrinkles, dryness, and impaired skin barrier function. Sirtuin-1 (SIRT1) is an NAD+-dependent protein deacetylase that suppresses cellular senescence and matrix metalloproteinase (MMP) expression, both of which contribute to skin thinning. Here, we investigated whether dietary resveratrol (RSV), a SIRT1 activator, mitigates age-related skin thinning in mice. Epidermal and dermal thicknesses were significantly reduced in 60-week-old (wo) mice compared with 20-wo mice, and these changes were attenuated by RSV administration (0.4 g/kg diet) from 28 weeks of age for 32 weeks. In the epidermis, the number of Ki67-positive cells decreased in aged mice but was restored by RSV treatment. Although RSV did not alter the number of SIRT1-positive cells, it significantly reduced the number of acetyl-lysine-positive cells, indicating enhanced SIRT1 activity. In the dermis, the immunopositive areas of MMP-9 and MMP-13 increased in aged mice, and these age-associated increases were not observed in RSV-treated aged mice. Phosphorylated SMAD2 levels decreased with age but were not affected by RSV. Furthermore, SIRT1 knockdown in human HaCaT keratinocytes increased p21 expression and senescence-associated β-galactosidase activity. Taken together, these findings suggest that the SIRT1 activator RSV ameliorates age-related skin thinning by suppressing cellular senescence in the epidermis and inhibiting dermal collagen degradation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Aging is associated with thinning of the epidermis and dermis, leading to wrinkles, dryness, and impaired skin barrier function. The study aimed to investigate whether dietary resveratrol (RSV), a SIRT1 activator, mitigates age-related skin thinning in mice.
Design and population
The study was conducted on mice, with epidermal and dermal thicknesses measured in 20-wo and 60-wo mice. RSV administration was tested in 60-wo mice starting at 28 weeks of age for 32 weeks.
Core methods
Epidermal and dermal thicknesses were measured using histological analysis. The number of Ki67-positive cells and acetyl-lysine-positive cells was assessed via immunohistochemistry. Phosphorylated SMAD2 levels were evaluated using Western blot. SIRT1 knockdown in human HaCaT keratinocytes was performed using siRNA, and p21 expression and senescence-associated β-galactosidase activity were measured.
Main results
Epidermal and dermal thicknesses were significantly reduced in 60-week-old mice compared with 20-week-old mice, and these changes were attenuated by RSV administration. In the epidermis, the number of Ki67-positive cells decreased in aged mice but was restored by RSV treatment. The number of acetyl-lysine-positive cells was significantly reduced by RSV, indicating enhanced SIRT1 activity. In the dermis, the immunopositive areas of MMP-9 and MMP-13 increased in aged mice, but these age-associated increases were not observed in RSV-treated aged mice. Phosphorylated SMAD2 levels decreased with age but were not affected by RSV.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings suggest that the SIRT1 activator RSV ameliorates age-related skin thinning by suppressing cellular senescence in the epidermis and inhibiting dermal collagen degradation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform future research on the role of SIRT1 in age-related skin changes and the potential use of SIRT1 activators in therapeutic interventions for skin aging.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to mice and does not account for human variability. The evidence is based on histological and biochemical analyses, and the results are interpreted as suggesting a mechanism rather than establishing causality. The confidence in the findings is moderate.

2021-2023 年俄罗斯新西伯利亚住院儿童呼吸道病毒感染的分子 流行病学

Molecular epidemiology of respiratory viral infections in hospitalized children in Novosibirsk, Russia, 2021–2023
作者： Denis Evgenievich Maslov, Ivan Dmitrievich Osipov, Daria Sergeevna Zabelina, Diana Sergeevna Demina, Yulia Eduardovna Tomilova, Sabokhat Bakhromovna Berdieva, Viktoriya Viktorovna Makukha, Tatiana Valerievna Komissarova, Eduard Felixovich Agletdinov, Sergey Viktorovich Netesov
摘要中文翻译背景 急性呼吸道感染 (ARIs) 是全球儿童发病和 死亡率 的主要原因之一。虽然 ARI 的病毒因子受到严格监测,但其他病原生物和病原体(宿主 微生物组原生的细菌和真菌)仍然在很大程度上被忽视。腺病毒类型也值得特别关注,特别是考虑到腺病毒 媒介 疫苗 在 COVID-19 大流行期间的广泛使用。方法 诊断小组对 2021 年 2 月至 2023 年 6 月期间在俄罗斯新西伯利亚住院的 1679 名 18 岁以下儿童采集的口鼻拭子进行了针对 17 种病毒、12 种细菌和 1 种酵母菌的核酸检测。腺病毒阳性样本还通过桑格测序进行精细分型。结果 总体而言,79.15% 的样本对 17 种病毒病原体中的至少一种呈阳性,其中 RSV(22.87% 阳性)和 RhV(24.54% 阳性)在病原学结构中占主导地位。呼吸道病原体和机会性病原体的口鼻存在率分别为 67.96% 和 11.49%。一般来说,病毒季节性是大陆性气候的典型特征,只有两个明显的偏差:2022 年春季没有 MpV,随后同年 12 月初出现峰值;RSV 峰值较早出现在 2021 年 10 月,并在下一个流行季节扩大流通。总体而言,所分析病毒的 流行病学 与全球 监测 趋势一致,并且在随后的两个流行季节之间基本稳定。在病毒-细菌关联分析中,我们发现了两个显着吸引的对,其中病毒已知促进细菌粘附到呼吸道上皮。腺病毒精细分型提供了初步证据,表明血清型1、2、3和7是分型样本中最常检测到的,而疫苗血清型AdV-5仅在两例中被鉴定出。结论 广泛的目标覆盖范围可以对流行病学模式进行全面分析,突出病原学结构稳定至大流行前水平的趋势,并可以优先考虑临床相关的病毒-细菌关联。腺病毒精细分型的初步结果与血清型 患病率 的全球趋势一致,有助于解决区域腺病毒 流行病学 的重要差距。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
急性呼吸道感染 (ARIs) 是全球儿童发病和 死亡率 的主要原因,但呼吸道病原体和机会性病原体以及病毒因子的作用仍未得到充分研究。
设计与对象
这项研究前瞻性招募了 2021 年 2 月至 2023 年 6 月期间在俄罗斯新西伯利亚住院的 1679 名 18 岁以下儿童,利用综合诊断小组调查呼吸道病毒和细菌病因。
核心方法
通过核酸检测,对口鼻拭子进行了17种病毒、12种细菌和1种酵母菌的检测;腺病毒阳性样本经过桑格测序进行精细分型。
主要结果
22.87% 的样本中检测到 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV),使其成为仅次于鼻病毒 (24.54%) 的第二常见病毒病原体,总体病毒阳性率为 79.15%。
解释与创新
观察到的 RSV 流行病学 与全球 监测 趋势一致,并显示出大流行前模式的稳定,初步腺病毒血清分型表明 1、2、3 和 7 型占主导地位。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持持续的 RSV 监测 并强调需要监测腺病毒血清型循环,特别是在腺病毒 媒介 疫苗 使用的情况下。
局限与证据强度
该研究的单中心设计和缺乏纵向随访限制了普遍性和因果推断,需要进行适度的置信度评估。

Molecular epidemiology of respiratory viral infections in hospitalized children in Novosibirsk, Russia, 2021–2023

Authors: Denis Evgenievich Maslov, Ivan Dmitrievich Osipov, Daria Sergeevna Zabelina, Diana Sergeevna Demina, Yulia Eduardovna Tomilova, Sabokhat Bakhromovna Berdieva, Viktoriya Viktorovna Makukha, Tatiana Valerievna Komissarova, Eduard Felixovich Agletdinov, Sergey Viktorovich Netesov
Original abstract (English)
Background Acute respiratory infections (ARIs) are among the leading global causes of morbidity and mortality in children. While viral agents of ARI are rigorously monitored, other pathogenic organisms and pathobionts — bacteria and fungi native to the host microbiome — remain largely neglected. Adenoviral types also warrant particular attention especially given the widespread use of adenoviral vector vaccines during the COVID-19 pandemic. Methods Oro-nasal swabs collected from 1679 children under the age of 18 hospitalized in Novosibirsk, Russia between February 2021 and June 2023, were tested for the presence of nucleic acids by the diagnostic panel targeting 17 viruses, 12 bacteria and one yeast. Adenovirus-positive samples were additionally fine-typed by Sanger sequencing. Results Overall, 79.15% of samples were positive for at least one of 17 viral pathogens, with RSV (22.87% positives) and RhV (24.54% positives) dominating the etiological structure. Oro-nasal presence for respiratory pathobionts and opportunistic pathogens was 67.96% and 11.49%, respectively. Generally, viral seasonality was typical for continental climate, with only two notable deviations: MpV absence in spring of 2022 followed by earlier December peaks in the same year; an earlier RSV maximum in October 2021 with extended circulation the next epidemic season. Overall, the epidemiology of the analyzed viruses was consistent with worldwide surveillance trends and largely stable between the two later epidemic seasons. In viral-bacterial association analysis we identified two significantly attracted pairs, in which virus is known to facilitate bacterial adherence to respiratory epithelium. Adenovirus fine-typing provided preliminary evidence that serotypes 1, 2, 3, and 7 are the most frequently detected among the typed samples, while vaccine serotype AdV-5 was identified in only two cases. Conclusion Broad target coverage allowed for a comprehensive analysis of epidemiological patterns, highlighting a trend toward stabilization of the etiological structure to pre-pandemic levels, and allowed for prioritization of clinically relevant viral-bacterial associations. Preliminary findings in adenoviral fine-typing are consistent with global trends in serotype prevalence and help address an important gap in regional adenoviral epidemiology.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Acute respiratory infections (ARIs) are a major cause of childhood morbidity and mortality globally, yet the role of respiratory pathobionts and opportunistic pathogens alongside viral agents remains understudied.
Design and population
This study prospectively enrolled 1679 children under 18 years hospitalized in Novosibirsk, Russia between February 2021 and June 2023 to investigate respiratory viral and bacterial etiologies using a comprehensive diagnostic panel.
Core methods
Oro-nasal swabs were tested for 17 viruses, 12 bacteria, and one yeast using nucleic acid detection; adenovirus-positive samples underwent Sanger sequencing for fine-typing.
Main results
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) was detected in 22.87% of samples, making it the second most common viral pathogen after rhinovirus (24.54%), with viral positivity overall at 79.15%.
Interpretation and novelty
The observed RSV epidemiology aligned with global surveillance trends and showed stabilization toward pre-pandemic patterns, with preliminary adenoviral serotyping indicating dominance of types 1, 2, 3, and 7.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support continued RSV surveillance and highlight the need to monitor adenoviral serotype circulation, particularly in the context of adenoviral vector vaccine use.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s single-centre design and lack of longitudinal follow-up limit generalizability and causal inference, warranting a moderate confidence assessment.

使用白藜芦醇和病毒溶瘤治疗弥漫性内源性脑桥胶质瘤的新型组合治疗策略:一项体外研究

A novel combinatorial therapeutic strategy using resveratrol and viral oncolysis for diffuse intrinsic pontine glioma: an in vitro study
作者： Karthik Gourishetti, Isaiah Davis, Shiv Patel, Karthik Rangavajhula, Christian Jacobsen, Monique Ruschel Kanitz, Darshini Kuruppu, Deepak Bhere
摘要中文翻译摘要 小儿中枢神经系统 (CNS) 肿瘤,特别是弥漫性中线胶质瘤 (DMG) 是儿童癌症相关死亡的主要原因,对新疗法的开发提出了未满足的需求。在这项研究中,我们评估了白藜芦醇 (RSV) 和溶瘤单纯疱疹病毒 (oHSV) hrR3 在患者来源的弥漫性脑桥胶质瘤 (DIPG) 细胞系中的联合作用。RSV 或 hrR3 单一疗法导致细胞活力呈剂量和时间依赖性降低。RSV 预处理后进行 hrR3 感染,与单一疗法相比,肿瘤细胞活力显着降低。RSV 预处理进一步增强了 hrR3 的复制,病毒滴度增加证明了这一点。机制分析表明,联合治疗与信号转导子和转录激活子 3 (STAT3) 和蛋白激酶 B (AKT) 信号通路的磷酸化降低以及细胞凋亡标记物的表达增加相关,包括裂解 caspase-3 和聚 (ADP-核糖) 聚合酶 (PARP)。这些发现共同表明,RSV 在体外通过增强病毒复制、抑制生存信号传导和促进 DIPG 细胞凋亡来增强 DIPG 细胞中 hrR3 介导的溶瘤作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探索一种针对弥漫性中线胶质瘤(DMG)的新治疗策略,特别是弥漫性内源性脑桥胶质瘤(DIPG),它是儿童癌症相关死亡的主要原因,并且对新 治疗 的需求尚未得到满足。
设计与对象
该研究是一项体外研究,评估白藜芦醇 (RSV) 和溶瘤单纯疱疹病毒 (oHSV) hrR3 在患者来源的 DIPG 细胞系中的联合作用。
核心方法
该研究评估了 RSV 和 hrR3 单一疗法及其组合对 DIPG 细胞活力的影响,机制分析侧重于信号通路和凋亡标志物。
主要结果
与单一疗法相比,RSV 预处理后进行 hrR3 感染显着降低了肿瘤细胞活力,观察到病毒滴度增加以及 STAT3 和 AKT 信号通路磷酸化减少。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,RSV 通过促进病毒复制和诱导细胞凋亡来增强 DIPG 细胞中 hrR3 介导的溶瘤功效。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为 DIPG 的潜在治疗策略提供了见解,强调了 RSV 在增强体外溶瘤病毒有效性方面的作用。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外研究结果,证据中等,因为它不包括体内或临床数据。

A novel combinatorial therapeutic strategy using resveratrol and viral oncolysis for diffuse intrinsic pontine glioma: an in vitro study

Authors: Karthik Gourishetti, Isaiah Davis, Shiv Patel, Karthik Rangavajhula, Christian Jacobsen, Monique Ruschel Kanitz, Darshini Kuruppu, Deepak Bhere
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Pediatric central nervous system (CNS) tumors, specifically Diffuse Midline Gliomas (DMG) are a leading cause of cancer-related deaths in children presenting an unmet need for novel therapeutic development. In this study, we assessed the combined effects of resveratrol (RSV) and the oncolytic herpes simplex virus (oHSV), hrR3 in patient-derived diffuse intrinsic pontine glioma (DIPG) cell lines. RSV or hrR3 monotherapy resulted in a dose- and time-dependent reduction in cell viability. RSV pretreatment followed by hrR3 infection led to a significantly greater reduction in tumor cell viability than monotherapy. RSV pretreatment further enhanced the replication of hrR3, as evidenced by increased viral titers. Mechanistic analysis showed that the combination therapy was associated with reduced phosphorylation of the signal transducer and activator of transcription 3 (STAT3) and protein kinase B (AKT) signaling pathways, along with increased expression of apoptotic markers, including cleaved caspase-3 and Poly (ADP-ribose) polymerase (PARP). Together these findings indicate that RSV potentiates hrR3-mediated oncolysis in DIPG cells by enhancing viral replication, suppressing survival signaling and promoting apoptosis in DIPG cells in vitro.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to explore a novel therapeutic strategy for Diffuse Midline Gliomas (DMG), specifically Diffuse Intrinsic Pontine Glioma (DIPG), which are a leading cause of cancer-related deaths in children and present an unmet need for new treatments.
Design and population
The study was an in vitro investigation assessing the combined effects of resveratrol (RSV) and the oncolytic herpes simplex virus (oHSV), hrR3, in patient-derived DIPG cell lines.
Core methods
The study evaluated the effects of RSV and hrR3 monotherapy and their combination on DIPG cell viability, with mechanistic analysis focusing on signaling pathways and apoptotic markers.
Main results
RSV pretreatment followed by hrR3 infection significantly reduced tumor cell viability compared to monotherapy, with increased viral titers and reduced phosphorylation of STAT3 and AKT signaling pathways observed.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that RSV enhances the efficacy of hrR3-mediated oncolysis in DIPG cells by promoting viral replication and inducing apoptosis.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into potential therapeutic strategies for DIPG, highlighting the role of RSV in augmenting oncolytic virus effectiveness in vitro.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro findings, and the evidence is moderate as it does not include in vivo or clinical data.

严重 RSV 肺炎与侵袭性肺曲霉病之间的关联

Association between severe RSV pneumonia and invasive pulmonary aspergillosis
作者： Jarom Heijmans, Josje Altenburg, Marije K. Bomers, Marcella C.A. Müller, Karin van Dijk, Marije Bomers, Marcella Müller
摘要中文翻译方法在2016年1月1日至2024年12月31日期间进行的一项回顾性队列研究研究中,纳入了入住重症监护病房(ICU)接受呼吸支持并接受支气管肺泡灌洗且RSV阳性且有临床信息的患者。结果 我们确定了 16 名 ICU 患者因 RSV 肺炎而接受呼吸支持。伦理声明 伦理批准 本研究由荷兰阿姆斯特丹阿姆斯特丹UMC医学伦理审查委员会根据赫尔辛基宣言批准。摘要 目的 侵袭性肺曲霉病(IPA)是一种危及生命的机会性感染,最常见于严重免疫功能低下的患者。在没有经典 宿主 因素(例如中性粒细胞减少症)的患者中,已在患有流感和 COVID-19 的危重患者中描述了 IPA。在过去的几年中,IPA 与其他呼吸道病毒之间存在关联,特别是在免疫功能低下的 宿主 中。在我们研究所,我们最近观察到多例呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)感染和IPA的危重患者,大多数是接受过异体血液干细胞移植(HSCT)的患者。我们评估了 RSV 感染危重患者的 患病率 和 IPA 的潜在危险因素。分析了有关基础疾病、放射学、真菌学测试和 死亡率 的数据。其中,5 名患者 (31%) 可能患有 IPA,他们都接受了同种异体 SCT。这些患者的 IPA 30 天 死亡率 为 100%。与 RSV 阴性患者相比,同种异体 HSCT 和严重 RSV 患者的 IPA 患病率 有所增加,尽管不显着(56% vs. 结论 IPA 与 RSV 相关)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 RSV 感染危重患者的 患病率 和侵袭性肺曲霉病 (IPA) 的潜在危险因素,因为 IPA 先前已与免疫功能低下的 宿主 中的其他呼吸道病毒相关。
设计与对象
一项回顾性 队列研究 研究于 2016 年 1 月 1 日至 2024 年 12 月 31 日期间进行,研究对象包括入住重症监护病房 (ICU) 接受呼吸支持且支气管肺泡灌洗 RSV 呈阳性并具有可用临床信息的患者。
核心方法
该研究分析了因 RSV 感染而接受支气管肺泡灌洗并入住 ICU 接受呼吸支持的患者的基础疾病、放射学、真菌学检测和 Z​​XQTERMRQXZ 数据。
主要结果
16 名 ICU 患者因 RSV 肺炎接受呼吸支持,其中 5 名 (31%) 可能患有 IPA,所有 5 名患者均接受了同种异体干细胞移植。这些患者的 30 天 IPA 死亡率 率为 100%。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,与 RSV 相关的 IPA 似乎主要发生在同种异体 HSCT 受者中,且预后较差,这突显了这一特定患者群体中先前未报告的关联。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,在患有严重 RSV 感染的免疫功能低下患者中,特别是那些接受过同种异体 HSCT 的患者中,IPA 应被视为潜在的并发症,从而为针对性的 监测 和诊断策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和潜在的选择偏倚而受到限制,RSV 阳性和 RSV 阴性患者之间 IPA 患病率 的比较不显着。由于这些限制,证据是中等的。

Association between severe RSV pneumonia and invasive pulmonary aspergillosis

Authors: Jarom Heijmans, Josje Altenburg, Marije K. Bomers, Marcella C.A. Müller, Karin van Dijk, Marije Bomers, Marcella Müller
Original abstract (English)
Methods In a retrospective cohort study between January 1st 2016 and December 31st 2024 patients who had been admitted to the intensive care unit (ICU) for respiratory support and underwent a bronchoalveolar lavage that was positive for RSV of whom clinical information was available, were included. Results We identified 16 ICU patients that received respiratory support due to an RSV pneumonia. Ethics declarations Ethical approval This study was approved by the medical ethics review committee of the AmsterdamUMC, Amsterdam, The Netherlands in accordance with the Declaration of Helsinki. Abstract Objectives Invasive pulmonary aspergillosis (IPA) is a life-threatening opportunistic infection, most often seen in severely immunocompromised patients. In patients without classical host factors such as neutropenia, IPA has been described in critically ill patients with influenza and COVID-19. In past years, associations between IPA and other respiratory viruses have been made, especially in immunocompromised hosts. In our institute, we have recently observed multiple critically ill patients with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection and IPA, mostly in patients who had undergone allogeneic hematological stem cell transplantation (HSCT). We evaluated the prevalence and potential risk factors of IPA in critically ill patients with an RSV infection. Data regarding underlying disease, radiology, mycological tests, and mortality were analyzed. Of these, five patients (31%) had probable IPA, all of whom had undergone an allogeneic SCT. The 30-day mortality of IPA in these patients was 100%. Patients with an allogeneic HSCT and severe RSV had increased prevalence of IPA compared to RSV negative patients, albeit non-significantly (56% vs. Conclusions IPA associated with RSV appea...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the prevalence and potential risk factors of invasive pulmonary aspergillosis (IPA) in critically ill patients with an RSV infection, as IPA has been previously associated with other respiratory viruses in immunocompromised hosts.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study was conducted between January 1st 2016 and December 31st 2024, including patients admitted to the intensive care unit (ICU) for respiratory support and who had a bronchoalveolar lavage positive for RSV with available clinical information.
Core methods
The study analyzed data on underlying disease, radiology, mycological tests, and mortality from patients who had undergone bronchoalveolar lavage for RSV infection and were admitted to the ICU for respiratory support.
Main results
Sixteen ICU patients received respiratory support due to RSV pneumonia, of whom five (31%) had probable IPA, and all five had undergone allogeneic stem cell transplantation. The 30-day mortality rate for IPA in these patients was 100%.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that IPA associated with RSV appears to occur primarily in post-allogeneic HSCT recipients and has a poor prognosis, highlighting a previously unreported association in this specific patient group.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that in immunocompromised patients with severe RSV infection, particularly those who have undergone allogeneic HSCT, IPA should be considered as a potential complication, informing targeted surveillance and diagnostic strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for selection bias, with a non-significant comparison between RSV-positive and RSV-negative patients for IPA prevalence. The evidence is moderate due to these limitations.

青春期前非肥胖男孩的小儿假性脑瘤综合征:无症状视力障碍和正常脑部 MRI - 病例报告

PEDIATRIC PSEUDOTUMOR CEREBRI SYNDROME IN A PREPUBERTAL NON-OBESE BOY: SILENT VISUAL IMPAIRMENT AND A NORMAL BRAIN MRI - A CASE REPORT
作者： Bartosz Wójtowicz, Szymon Skrzypek, Patrycja Okoń, Jagoda Niczyporuk, Monika Zajączkowska, Ilona Kozioł, Magdalena Chrościńska–Krawczyk
摘要中文翻译背景:假性脑瘤综合征(PTCS)的特点是在脑脊液成分正常和神经影像学正常或典型改变的情况下,一旦排除次要原因,颅内压就会升高。在成人中,这种疾病通常影响年轻的肥胖女性,但在儿童中,人口统计学特征更加异质,青春期前的患者通常不肥胖且性别不一。早期识别至关重要,因为未经治疗的 PTCS 可能会导致不可逆的视力丧失,但当临床表现偏离成人刻板印象时,这种情况很容易被忽视。病例介绍:一名6岁10个月的青春期前男孩,有反复上呼吸道感染史,有三周腹痛、头痛、呕吐和复视病史。体重指数为 13.67 kg/m2(非肥胖)。眼底镜检查发现双侧视盘水肿,右眼视力轻度下降至0.7,左眼视力轻度下降至0.5,但孩子没有报告视力症状。大脑、眼眶和整个脊柱的磁共振成像没有异常,低位小脑扁桃体的计算机断层扫描结果也没有得到 MRI 的证实。诊断性腰椎穿刺产生透明、无色的脑脊液,生化正常,感染和自身免疫检查结果呈阴性。乙酰唑胺、静脉注射甘露醇和腰椎穿刺治疗后,视力完全恢复(每只眼 1.0),两周内视乳头水肿消退。由于疑似细菌重复感染,呼吸道合胞病毒 接受了支持治疗和阿莫西林克拉维酸治疗。讨论:该案例说明了三个教学要点:无论性别,青春期前非肥胖儿童都必须考虑PTCS;视力障碍可能会悄无声息地发展,并通过提示 治疗 完全恢复;正常的脑 MRI 并不排除 诊断,它依赖于临床特征、视乳头水肿、脑脊液检查结果和排除模仿的结合。结论:对于任何有提示性症状的儿童,无论其人口特征如何,临床医生都应保持较低的眼底镜检查和腰椎穿刺阈值,因为即使儿童没有报告视力症状,及时的 治疗 也可以防止永久性视力丧失。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
假性脑瘤综合征(PTCS)是一种颅内压升高、脑脊液成分正常的神经系统疾病,但其在青春期前非肥胖儿童中的识别具有挑战性,因为临床表现常常偏离肥胖年轻女性的成人刻板印象。该研究强调需要早期使用 诊断 来预防不可逆的视力丧失,特别是当症状微妙或不典型时。
设计与对象
这是一名 6 岁 10 个月的青春期前非肥胖男孩的单一患者 病例报告,其表现为头痛、呕吐、腹痛和复视。该患者有反复上呼吸道感染病史,并在进行诊断性腰椎穿刺和神经影像学检查的临床环境中接受治疗。
核心方法
诊断评估包括眼底镜检查以评估视盘水肿、大脑和眼眶的磁共振成像、大脑计算机断层扫描、腰椎穿刺进行脑脊液分析以及排除感染性和自身免疫性原因。治疗 涉及乙酰唑胺、静脉注射甘露醇和治疗性腰椎穿刺。
主要结果
眼底镜检查显示双侧视盘水肿,视力轻度下降(右眼0.7,左眼0.5)。大脑和眼眶的磁共振成像没有异常,脑脊液分析显示生化正常,没有感染或自身免疫的证据。治疗后,双眼视力完全恢复至1.0,视乳头水肿在两周内消退。
解释与创新
该病例表明,不论性别,青春期前非肥胖儿童都可能发生 PTCS,视力障碍可能会悄无声息地发生,并且正常的脑部 MRI 并不排除 诊断。新颖之处在于强调即时识别和 治疗 的重要性,以防止永久性视力丧失,即使症状不是明显可见。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,无论人口特征如何,临床医生都需要对有提示症状的儿童维持较低的眼底镜检查和腰椎穿刺阈值,以避免 诊断 延迟和视力丧失等不可逆转的并发症。
局限与证据强度
作为单一的病例报告,证据的普遍性有限,无法建立因果关系或患病率。由于缺乏比较数据、对照或更广泛的人群抽样,证据的强度较低。

PEDIATRIC PSEUDOTUMOR CEREBRI SYNDROME IN A PREPUBERTAL NON-OBESE BOY: SILENT VISUAL IMPAIRMENT AND A NORMAL BRAIN MRI - A CASE REPORT

Authors: Bartosz Wójtowicz, Szymon Skrzypek, Patrycja Okoń, Jagoda Niczyporuk, Monika Zajączkowska, Ilona Kozioł, Magdalena Chrościńska–Krawczyk
Original abstract (English)
Background: Pseudotumor cerebri syndrome (PTCS) is characterized by elevated intracranial pressure in the setting of normal cerebrospinal fluid composition and normal or typically altered neuroimaging, once secondary causes have been excluded. In adults the disease classically affects young obese women, but in children the demographic profile is more heterogeneous, with prepubertal patients frequently being non-obese and of either sex. Early recognition is essential because untreated PTCS can cause irreversible visual loss, yet the condition is easily overlooked when the clinical presentation deviates from the adult stereotype. Case presentation: A 6-year-10-month-old prepubertal boy with a history of recurrent upper respiratory tract infections presented with a three-week history of abdominal pain, headache, vomiting, and double vision. Body mass index was 13.67 kg/m2 (non-obese). Fundoscopy disclosed bilateral optic disc edema, and visual acuity was mildly reduced to 0.7 in the right eye and 0.5 in the left eye, although the child did not report visual symptoms. Magnetic resonance imaging of the brain, orbits, and whole spine was unremarkable, and computed tomography findings of low-lying cerebellar tonsils were not confirmed on MRI. Diagnostic lumbar puncture yielded clear, colorless cerebrospinal fluid with normal biochemistry and negative infectious and autoimmune workup. Acetazolamide, intravenous mannitol, and therapeutic lumbar puncture were followed by complete visual recovery (1.0 in each eye) and regression of papilledema within two weeks. Respiratory syncytial virus was treated with supportive care and amoxicillin-clavulanate because of suspected bacterial superinfection. Discussion: This case illustrates three teaching points: PTCS must be considered in prepubertal non-obese children regardless of sex; visual impairment may develop silently and recover fully with prompt treatment; and a normal brain MRI does not exclude the diagnosis, which rests on the combination of clinical features, papilledema, cerebrospinal fluid findings, and exclusion of mimics. Conclusion: Clinicians should maintain a low threshold for fundoscopy and lumbar puncture in any child with suggestive symptoms, irrespective of demographic profile, because timely treatment can prevent permanent visual loss even when the child does not report visual symptoms.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Pseudotumor cerebri syndrome (PTCS) is a neurological condition with elevated intracranial pressure and normal cerebrospinal fluid composition, yet its recognition in prepubertal non-obese children is challenging because clinical presentations often deviate from the adult stereotype of obese young women. The study highlights the need for early diagnosis to prevent irreversible visual loss, particularly when symptoms are subtle or atypical.
Design and population
This is a single-patient case report of a 6-year-10-month-old prepubertal non-obese boy presenting with headache, vomiting, abdominal pain, and double vision. The patient had a history of recurrent upper respiratory tract infections and was managed in a clinical setting where diagnostic lumbar puncture and neuroimaging were performed.
Core methods
Diagnostic evaluation included fundoscopy to assess optic disc edema, magnetic resonance imaging of the brain and orbits, computed tomography of the brain, lumbar puncture for cerebrospinal fluid analysis, and exclusion of infectious and autoimmune causes. Treatment involved acetazolamide, intravenous mannitol, and therapeutic lumbar puncture.
Main results
Fundoscopy revealed bilateral optic disc edema with mildly reduced visual acuity (0.7 right eye, 0.5 left eye). Magnetic resonance imaging of the brain and orbits was unremarkable, and cerebrospinal fluid analysis showed normal biochemistry with no evidence of infection or autoimmunity. Following treatment, visual acuity recovered fully to 1.0 in both eyes, and papilledema regressed within two weeks.
Interpretation and novelty
The case demonstrates that PTCS can occur in prepubertal non-obese children regardless of sex, that visual impairment may develop silently, and that a normal brain MRI does not exclude the diagnosis. The novelty lies in emphasizing the importance of prompt recognition and treatment to prevent permanent visual loss even when symptoms are not overtly visual.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the need for clinicians to maintain a low threshold for fundoscopy and lumbar puncture in children with suggestive symptoms, irrespective of demographic profile, to avoid delayed diagnosis and irreversible complications such as visual loss.
Limitations and evidence strength
As a single-case report, the evidence is limited in generalizability and cannot establish causality or prevalence. The strength of the evidence is low due to the lack of comparative data, controls, or broader population sampling.
